While the victim is imprisoned, they will have no knowledge of anything that happens outside the prison, nor does anyone outside have knowledge of the prisoner. To break free, the prison is considered a damage resistant creature with AT 10, with a number of life points based on the level of the spell. However, any damage suffered from the outside is doubled automatically. The prison additionally recovers from damage with a regeneration value of 19. If the target of the spell fails an MR check, they are imprisoned and do not have the opportunity for a subsequent roll. If they wish to leave, they must break free from the prison. The caster may not lock themselves into their own Prison of Light."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "One with the Light Light 96 Permits the caster to enter a state of utter meditation where their body becomes one with the Light. They abandon this world and ascend into the flow of souls to nourish themselves from its energies, apart from, and without knowledge of anything happening elsewhere. In this state, they multiply their Zeon Regeneration rate by 10, and cure their wounds with a regeneration of 16. Although there is no maintenance cost for this spell, the caster can be in the flow of souls for a maximum time period based on the level of the spell.":
        - cell "One with the Light"
        - cell "Light"
        - cell "96"

    - rowgroup:

        - cell "Light"
        - cell "98"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Holocaust of Light Light 100 Unleashes the power of Light in its purest state, sweeping away everything in the spiritual as well as material world. The freed energy sweeps up and devours everything, unifying its existence to the Light. Even elemental creatures of light are assimilated by its power. The Holocaust of Light creates a great luminous dome within which everything is dissolved. It is an Energy attack with damage and radius determined by the level of the spell. Anyone receiving damage, regardless of the amount, must pass an MR check with difficulty 160 or be joined with the light, and automatically be destroyed in both body and soul. It is not possible to designate targets within the radius of this spell; all except the caster are equally affected.":
        - cell "Holocaust of Light"
        - cell "Light"

        - cell "Encloses the victim in an inescapable world of darkness, a separate universe with no interaction with our own. To break free, the prison is considered a damage resistant creature with AT 10, with a number of life points based on the level of the spell. However, any damage suffered from the outside is doubled automatically. The prison additionally recovers from damage with a regeneration value of 19. If the target of the spell fails an MR check, they are imprisoned and do not have the opportunity for a subsequent roll. If they wish to leave, they must break free from the prison. The caster may not lock themselves into their own Prison of Darkness."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "One with the Dark Darkness 96 The caster enters a state of utter meditation where their body becomes one with the dark. They abandon this world and ascend into the flow of souls to nourish themselves from its energies, apart from, and without knowledge of anything happening elsewhere. In this state, they multiply their Zeon Regeneration rate by 10, and cure their wounds with Regeneration 16. Although there is no maintenance cost for this spell, the caster can be in the flow of souls for a time period determined by the level of the spell.":
        - cell "One with the Dark"
        - cell "Darkness"
        - cell "96"
        - cell "The caster enters a state of utter meditation where their body becomes one with the dark. They abandon this world and ascend into the flow of souls to nourish themselves from its energies, apart from, and without knowledge of anything happening elsewhere. In this state, they multiply their Zeon Regeneration rate by 10, and cure their wounds with Regeneration 16. Although there is no maintenance cost for this spell, the caster can be in the flow of souls for a time period determined by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Dark Ascension Darkness 98 Exchanges the material essence of a person for divine energy, modifying their spirit through pure supernatural power. While in this state, the caster or their target is imbued with elevated Gnosis determined by the level of the spell.":
        - cell "Dark Ascension"
        - cell "Darkness"
        - cell "98"
        - cell "Exchanges the material essence of a person for divine energy, modifying their spirit through pure supernatural power. While in this state, the caster or their target is imbued with elevated Gnosis determined by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Holocaust of Darkness Darkness 100 Unleashes the power of Darkness in its purest state, sweeping away everything in the spiritual as well as material world. The freed energy sweeps up and devours everything, unifying its existence to the Darkness. Even elemental creatures of Darkness are assimilated by its power. The Holocaust of Darkness creates a great shadowed dome within which everything is dissolved. It is an Energy attack with damage and radius determined by the level of the spell. Anyone receiving damage, regardless of the amount, must pass an MR check of difficulty 160 or be joined with the dark, and automatically be destroyed in both body and soul. It is not possible to designate targets within the radius of this spell; all except the caster are equally affected.":
        - cell "Holocaust of Darkness"
        - cell "Darkness"
        - cell "100"

It is not possible to designate targets within the radius of this spell; all except the caster are equally affected."

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Create Homunculus Creation 26 Homunculi are small magical beings that casters use at times for various purposes, from espionage to housework. The spell creates a number of mystical beings with 500 Development Points (Level 0), as per the rules in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings under complete control of the caster. They are a being between worlds with a Gnosis of 5, but given their minor nature, the following rules apply: • The characteristics of the being cannot exceed a score of 5. • Any primary and secondary abilities cannot exceed a score of 50. • Any Intellectual Secondary ability and Mystical ability chosen cannot exceed the ability of their creator. • They cannot have the Gift. • A special -2 penalty to size is applied, and the creature cannot have the Uncommon Size advantage. Outside of these limitations, each homunculi can be completely different."':
        - cell "Create Homunculus"
        - cell "Creation"
        - cell "26"
        - 'cell "Homunculi are small magical beings that casters use at times for various purposes, from espionage to housework. The spell creates a number of mystical beings with 500 Development Points (Level 0), as per the rules in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings under complete control of the caster. They are a being between worlds with a Gnosis of 5, but given their minor nature, the following rules apply: • The characteristics of the being cannot exceed a score of 5. • Any primary and secondary abilities cannot exceed a score of 50. • Any Intellectual Secondary ability and Mystical ability chosen cannot exceed the ability of their creator. • They cannot have the Gift. • A special -2 penalty to size is applied, and the creature cannot have the Uncommon Size advantage. Outside of these limitations, each homunculi can be completely different."'
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Minor Change Creation 28 Permits altering the appearance of an object or being by modifying its form. The change is limited to varying its exterior appearance, and does not alter it’s inherent qualities or nature. In this way, a shining short sword may become old and rusted, but never changed into a walking stick. In the case of living things, this spell can change the size of an individual by up to 2 points, and alter their physical appearance, such as making an ugly overweight man into a young and good looking one. The spell can be resisted with a successful MR check by individuals, and a new check may be repeated once per day. This spell may also affect a number of individuals or objects, as long as their combined presence is within the limit determined by the level of the spell.":

        - cell "Creation"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Imitate Creation 30 Creates an exact copy of an already existing inorganic object within reach of the caster. It creates a perfect imitation of the original that retains all of it’s qualities, including any supernatural ones. The copied object must be within the presence limit determined by the level of the spell. The spell has no effect on animated creatures, but can affect mechanical constructs as long as they have no soul.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Immunity Creation 32 Permits the caster or the character designated by them to be immune to a number of intensities of a determined type, either Fire, Cold, or Electricity. If an attack is made using the type of energy to which the character is immune, each intensity of immunity reduces the base damage of the attack by 5 points. If a resistance check is required, each immunity provides a +5 bonus to such a check.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Reduction of Damage Creation 36 Affects the power of an attack, reducing the force of it’s impact. Upon being cast, this spell reduces the base damage of an attack, by a value determined by the level of the spell. If the damage of the attack is reduced to 0, the attack produces no effect, even if it successfully hits its target. This spell must be declared and cast before any dice are rolled for the attack. Any attack can only be affected by one Reduction of Damage spell at a time.":

        - cell "Creation"

Any attack can only be affected by one Reduction of Damage spell at a time."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Physical Control Creation 38 The caster is able to control the body of a being as though it were a simple puppet. Since the control is purely physical, and not spiritual, the subject being controlled is not obligated to use any supernatural abilities (such as Magic, Ki, or Psychic powers) against their will, although any natural fighting ability is able to be used by the controller. Resisting the spell requires a successful MR check. Once under the caster’s control, the subject has the ability to make a new check once per day, as well as any time they are ordered to do something entirely against their nature.":
        - cell "Physical Control"
        - cell "Creation"
        - cell "38"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Create Memories Creation 46 Permits creation of new memories within the target, without necessarily erasing existing ones. Most of the time, unless the modified memories are very deeply rooted to the person, they will feel slightly confused, but convinced that their memories are real. The caster determines what information they are trying to introduce, regardless of complexity or duration in the subjects mind. Resisting the spell requires a successful MR or PsR check. Although the spell has no maintenance, the individual affected may perform a new resistance check each time something makes them suspect that their memory may be inaccurate.":

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Acquire Powers Creation 50 Grants the caster, or the person on whom the spell is cast, the ability to acquire the abilities of supernatural beings. In Game terms, the character receives an additional amount of Development Points to acquire any of the powers listed in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings, as though they had a Gnosis of 25. These also temporarily affect the subject’s physical form. If, for example, someone obtained the powers of Natural Flight, it would be logical for them to have developed enormous wings. Characters can only be subject to one Acquire Powers spell at a time."':

        - cell "Creation"
        - cell "50"

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Create Monstrosity Creation 52 Create a magical being with the appearance of life, but completely under the control of the caster. This being is developed using the rules for a being between worlds described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings. The creature has a number of Development Points determined by the level of the spell, and has a Gnosis of 20. Regardless of the degree at which the spell is cast, the being cannot exceed the level of it’s creator, and doesn’t have the capacity to receive a soul, so one cannot be given to them through the spells Transmigrate Soul or Provide Soul of this path."':

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Spiritual Standstill Creation 58 As the name of the spell indicates, this preserves the spirit of the individual on whom it is cast in the same state as the moment it affects them. From that moment on, the affected person does not make any further MR or PsR checks, and remains unalterably in the same spiritual condition. This person cannot be affected by states of any kind, whether positive or negative. The target may resist the effects of the spell when it is cast by succeeding at an MR check. Naturally, once under the spell, the person afflicted may not make another check to free themselves of it’s effects.":

      - 'row "Reinforce Magic Creation 68 Adds strength to another spell either launched at the same time or that is being maintained. In game terms, it applies the following bonuses: • +20 to the difficulty of any Resistance value • +50% to the base damage of the spell (round down to the nearest multiple of 5) • +50% to the increase to resistances the spell provides (round down to the nearest multiple of 5) • +20% to any other numeric value present in the spell (with the exception of Gnosis values, which are not affected) Once this spell lapses, the added power fades as well."':

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Transmute Creation 70 The caster transforms an inorganic object, converting it permanently into a different object of similar spiritual power. This permits the caster to modify something within the presence limit described by the level of the spell into something else of equal or lesser spiritual power.         - cell "The caster transforms an inorganic object, converting it permanently into a different object of similar spiritual power.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Metamorphism Creation 72 The caster can transform a person or object into anything they wish (a mouse, a statue, etc.) as long as the Presence of the new form is not higher than the target’s original amount. Living things metamorphosed into non-living objects continue to live in their new forms, even if it happens that they cannot move or breathe. When the spell lapses, the individual or object returns to their original form. Metamorphism does not permit the acquisition of any new essential powers or abilities, except for those possessed by natural creatures with Gnosis 0. Resisting the effects of this spell requires a successful MR check. If unsuccessful, victims are only allowed a new check once per day.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Zone of Safety Creation 82 Creates a magical zone inside which no person or object can be harmed in any way by anyone or anything. A person within the zone could not, for instance, break down a door or even harm a cockroach by stepping on it by mistake. Zone of Safety automatically affects anyone within it’s area, including the caster. The affected areas radius is determined by the level of the spell, and it remains stationary once cast. To overcome the effect requires a successful MR check, and it is only possible to repeat the check once per day, even if they leave the zone and then re-enter it.":

        - cell "Creation"
        - cell "82"

To overcome the effect requires a successful MR check, and it is only possible to repeat the check once per day, even if they leave the zone and then re-enter it."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Maintain Magic Creation 86 Through the use of this spell, another existing spell is tied to the world in a more lasting way. In game terms, another spell is provided a pool of Zeon to pay for maintenance of the spell without drawing additional resources from the caster. This does not increase the level of the spell, only the maintenance of the spell as it exists is affected.":
        - cell "Maintain Magic"
        - cell "Creation"
        - cell "86"
        - cell "Through the use of this spell, another existing spell is tied to the world in a more lasting way. This does not increase the level of the spell, only the maintenance of the spell as it exists is affected."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Provide Soul Creation 88 The caster is able to create a complete soul, giving the breath of life to an object or body able to contain one. If the soul is provided to a magically engendered creature such as those developed by the Create Being spell, or by the Level 82 spells of the minor paths, Provide Soul makes it unnecessary to continue maintaining those beings with the caster’s Zeon. The creature thereby breaks any bond it has with its creator, obtaining free will just like any other being between worlds. Not all souls created by this spell are capable of entering the body into which they are introduced. Any soul created this way must be provided a body within the presence limit described by the level of the spell, and that body cannot already contain a living soul.":
        - cell "Provide Soul"
        - cell "Creation"
        - cell "88"
        - cell "The caster is able to create a complete soul, giving the breath of life to an object or body able to contain one. If the soul is provided to a magically engendered creature such as those developed by the Create Being spell, or by the Level 82 spells of the minor paths, Provide Soul makes it unnecessary to continue maintaining those beings with the caster’s Zeon. The creature thereby breaks any bond it has with its creator, obtaining free will just like any other being between worlds. Not all souls created by this spell are capable of entering the body into which they are introduced. Any soul created this way must be provided a body within the presence limit described by the level of the spell, and that body cannot already contain a living soul."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Greater Creation Creation 90 Gives the caster the gift of creation at their whim, awarding them a pool of Presence to distribute freely between their various constructions or objects. They can create any inanimate object they wish, from castles to mountains, as long as the presence of each object is within the limit described by the level of the spell. Like the other Creation spells, objects created in this way must be done so in a way logical to their nature.":

        - cell "Creation"

        - cell "Gives the caster the gift of creation at their whim, awarding them a pool of Presence to distribute freely between their various constructions or objects. Like the other Creation spells, objects created in this way must be done so in a way logical to their nature."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Eternal Magic Creation 92 As the name of the spell indicates, it affects another existing spell and greatly reduces (or even eliminates) the maintenance cost. If the spell in question has a Daily maintenance cost, Eternal Magic stabilizes it, making further maintenance unnecessary. The spell continues to be controlled by it’s caster, but will continue its effect even after their death. If the spell’s maintenance is instead ‘per combat turn’, it becomes one requiring Daily maintenance instead. A spell can only be affected by one Eternal Magic effect at a time, and thus a ‘per combat turn’ spell cannot be made first Daily, and then Eternal.":
        - cell "Eternal Magic"
        - cell "Creation"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Increase Weakness Destruction 16 Finds a being’s weak points, and makes them even weaker supernaturally. In game terms, it doubles the penalties an individual suffers for an existing weakness or vulnerability. For example, an elemental who is especially vulnerable to light would suffer quadruple damage, instead of the standard double damage for such an attack. A character weak to poison would find their PsnR reduced to one quarter it’s value, instead of the standard one half for such a disadvantage. To avoid these effects, the target must pass an MR check, and are only permitted another check when affected by their weaknesses.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Devouring Zone Destruction 76 Like a deadly vortex, the Devouring Zone spreads, shredding the essence of everything it finds in its path by slowly consuming it. Little by little, the things this spell reaches weaken until they are unmade, whether they are material objects or living beings. Every day the victims (either people or objects) remain within the area, they must pass a MR or PhR check, or temporarily have their presence reduced by 5 points. If their presence falls to 0 from this effect, they decompose, leaving little to no trace. Living beings also receive an All Action Penalty equivalent to twice the Presence points lost to this effect.     - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Destroy Capabilities Destruction 78 Destroys part of the abilities or powers of an individual, stripping them of those powers forever. The caster freely removes a number of Development Points from the affected person, removing them from any ability they desire. They could, for instance, cut the victim’s Attack ability by 30 Development Points, and the remaining amount could be from a secondary ability of the caster’s choice. It is also possible to destroy the supernatural powers of mystical creatures with this spell, using the point values in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings for reference. This power also allows the destruction of advantages bought with Creation Points, at a cost of 100 DP per CP removed.     - rowgroup:

To resist this effect, an affected individual must pass an MR check. The spell can either be used in a line, with a distance and difficulty for the check determined by the level of the spell, or focused into a single point, increasing the associated difficulty by 20. In spite of being a Spiritual spell, the cut in reality is visible to anyone, even someone incapable of seeing magic.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Destruction of Zeon Destruction 86 By directly affecting the essence of magic, this spell is capable of dissipating the power of another active spell, automatically diminishing it’s Zeon value by an amount determined by the level of the spell. If this diminishes the spell below it’s Zeon value for the matching grade of the spell, it is reduced to the next lowest level. If this reduces the spell below it’s base cost, the spell is instead dismissed.":
        - cell "Destruction of Zeon"
        - cell "Destruction"
        - cell "86"
        - cell "By directly affecting the essence of magic, this spell is capable of dissipating the power of another active spell, automatically diminishing it’s Zeon value by an amount determined by the level of the spell. If this diminishes the spell below it’s Zeon value for the matching grade of the spell, it is reduced to the next lowest level. If this reduces the spell below it’s base cost, the spell is instead dismissed."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Sweep from the Heavens Destruction 88 By destroying its very essence, this spell takes away the divine presence of an entity, temporarily transforming it into an earthly creature. In game terms, the creature affected by this spell has its Gnosis value reduced. That reduction affects what powers or abilities the entity has if they depend on Gnosis, and as such cannot use them while in their reduced state. Resisting the spell requires a successful MR check, and it cannot be repeated in the event of a failure while the spell is maintained.":
        - cell "Sweep from the Heavens"
        - cell "Destruction"
        - cell "88"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Raise Wind Air 2 Conjures a draft of wind with a maximum speed and width determined by the level of the spell. The caster needs to be in the open or in an area likely to experience such drafts. Once created, the length of the draft is 10 times the width.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Move Air 6 Allows the caster to move inanimate objects without physical contact. In Game terms, as long as the objects affected are within the weight limit, they can be moved by the caster with a flight speed of 10.":
        - cell "Move"
        - cell "Air"
        - cell "6"

      - row "Weight Reduction Air 10 Reduces the material or person’s body weight by a given amount, based on the level of the spell. Anything affected by this spell is reduced to a minimum of 1kg.":

        - cell "Air"

        - cell "Reduces the material or person’s body weight by a given amount, based on the level of the spell. Anything affected by this spell is reduced to a minimum of 1kg."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Stop Breathing Air 12 Anyone affected by this spell no longer needs to breathe, and is no longer affected by a lack of air. It can affect as many individuals as desired, as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell.":
        - cell "Stop Breathing"
        - cell "Air"
        - cell "12"
        - cell "Anyone affected by this spell no longer needs to breathe, and is no longer affected by a lack of air. It can affect as many individuals as desired, as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Free Motion Air 16 This target of this spell is able to move freely along any type of solid surface, completely untouched by gravity. Individuals under the spell are able to walk on water or run on walls and ceilings. The caster may apply this spell to multiple individuals, as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell.":

        - cell "Air"
        - cell "16"
        - cell "This target of this spell is able to move freely along any type of solid surface, completely untouched by gravity. The caster may apply this spell to multiple individuals, as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Air Blow Air 20 The caster unleashes a potent strike of air that can either hit a single long distance target or a group of people together over a distance. In the latter case, the width is determined by the level of the spell. The blast exerts pressure equivalent to an independent strength based on the level of the spell, and hitting a single target increases this value by +2. Though modest, a blast of air can cause real damage equivalent to twice the bonus of the blow’s strength, and uses the Impact attack type. Since this attack is caused merely by the air, only those who pass an Absurd (180) difficulty, or have the ability to otherwise see magic, can perceive of the attack at all.":
        - cell "Air Blow"
        - cell "Air"
        - cell "20"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Air Screen Air 22 Forms a barrier of air that offers protection from all kinds of attacks, except for those based on Electricity or Energy. In addition, the strong winds raised by the spell hinders any physical projectiles either fired and thrown by the enemy, causing a -50 penalty to the projectile’s final Attack value. The amount of life points the shield has is determined by the level of the spell.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Automatic Transportation Air 26 The target of this spell can be transported up to a distance determined by the level of the spell, allowing them to pass through any physical object such as walls or doors – provided they are not energy barriers. The caster may apply this to as many individuals as desired, as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell. Those that wish to resist this effect must succeed at an MR check. Those that are to be transported to an unnatural location (such as mid-air) may apply a +40 bonus to this resistance check.":

        - cell "Air"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Electrify Air 36 Electrifies physical bodies, inflicting strong discharges on any who touch them. Anyone coming into physical contact with an electrified body, including the caster, must succeed at a PhR check per combat turn they remain in contact with the object, or suffer a loss of life points equal to half the level of failure. For the purposes of resistance and weakness, this is considered an Electricity attack. The electrified body cannot be above certain requirements for presence and maximum size, determined by the level of the spell.":

        - cell "Air"

The electrified body cannot be above certain requirements for presence and maximum size, determined by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Air Cut Air 40 Produces a strong gust of wind capable of inflicting cuts on any surface it comes into contact with. The cut affects an area in a line where no targets can be selected within.       - row "Speed Air 42 Increases the target’s speed. In game terms, it doubles the movement value allowed to characters. The caster may apply this bonus to as many individuals as desired, as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Lightning Air 46 Sets off a lightning strike, with a base damage of 100. The caster can choose to strike a single target, or multiple. If a single target is chosen, a bonus is applied to the base damage. In the latter case, after hitting it’s target, the electricity bounces to nearby targets within a 15 meter radius, causing a new attack with identical characteristics. Although the caster cannot choose these additional targets, they themselves are immune from these attacks. If there are no targets within the area, the lightning either vanishes or harmlessly strikes the ground.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Ethereal Form Air 52 Transforms a designated body into one of air, rendering it intangible and therefore immune to all matter and attacks not able to damage energy. For as long as the spell lasts, the subject is only visible to individuals who succeed at a Notice check of Almost Impossible (240) difficulty, or a Search check of Very Difficult (140). Those who can see magic can also see the ethereal individual. Although unable to fully pass through physical matter, those affected can pass through any crack or gap that allows the passage of air.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Air Control Air 56 Endows the caster with dominion over air and any other gaseous substance within an area around them, determined by the level of the spell. The caster can manipulate the air and any gaseous current at will, for instance, they can deprive an entire area of air, or change the course of a tornado. If a being of air enters the area or it is cast upon them, the caster may attempt to control the creature. The creature can attempt to resist this effect with a successful MR check.":

        - cell "Air"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Defensive Movement Air 62 Allows the caster to move away from the reach of an attack. When cast, the spell uses the caster’s Magic Projection ability in place of their Dodge ability. The caster may only defend themselves this way against a maximum number of attacks per combat turn, determined by the level of the spell.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Teletransportation Air 66 The caster, or the character appointed by them, is transported up to a maximum distance set by the level of the spell. This spell allows those transported to pass through solid objects to reach their destination, provided they are not energy barriers. The desired destination of the spell must be well known by the caster to be transported exactly to its location, otherwise the destination is approximate. The caster may affect as many targets as desired, as long as their combined presence is within the limit determined by the level of the spell.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Immateriality Air 70 The designated body becomes completely immaterial, thus turning intangible to all non-energy based creatures, objects, and attacks. While remaining in this state, the subject in question will not be able to touch or be touched, and can pass through any material not of a supernatural origin. If cast upon an unwilling target, the target must pass an MR check to avoid it’s effects. Should they fail the check and become immaterial, they are entitled to a new check each day. The caster may affect as many targets as desired, so long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Relocate Magic Air 90 The caster is able to transport the magical sources supporting an active spell from one place to another. In this way, the caster can relocate spells that are place-oriented, individual-oriented, and object-oriented. A character casting this spell may relocate any maintained spell with a Zeon value lower than the limit, even if the spell is not their own. Maximum relocation distance is determined by the general rules for magic projection. When relocating a Spiritual type spell, all effects are treated exactly as if the spell was just cast, allowing those affected a new chance to defend themselves with their normal resistance check.":

The spell’s level determines it’s base damage, and it produces an Impact upon the target with a given Strength value, which staggers and pushes the target from the force of the impact."
    - rowgroup:
      - row:
        - cell "Liquid Control"
        - cell "Water"
        - cell "22"
        - cell "Grants the caster complete control over an amount of liquid mass in the nearby area. The caster may alter the physical characteristics of this fluid (such as color and clarity) as well as it’s solidity. The liquid can even be moved without contact. When used against an elemental based on water, the caster can attempt to control the creature unless it succeeds at an MR check. Creatures that fail this check may attempt a new check each day. In Addition, this spell can also manipulate the bloodstreams of living organisms. However, in these cases the victims may attempt either an MR or PhR check, and they receive a +40 bonus. This style of manipulation allows the caster to apply an all action penalty, as well as damage per combat turn, equal to half the level of failure for the check. Victims who fail the check for this style of manipulation are allowed a new check every 5 combat turns."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Freeze Emotions Water 26 Stops a person’s emotions, granting them psychological immunity to all emotional states (both positive and negative), with some exceptions. At Base, it cannot stop Pain, nor any state applied by supernatural entities. Additional effects are based on higher levels of the spell.":
        - cell "Freeze Emotions"
        - cell "Water"
        - cell "26"
        - cell "Stops a person’s emotions, granting them psychological immunity to all emotional states (both positive and negative), with some exceptions. At Base, it cannot stop Pain, nor any state applied by supernatural entities. Additional effects are based on higher levels of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Control Cold Water 30 Grants the caster the ability to control a number of cold intensities. Control Cold empowers the caster to alter the potency to half of it’s original value, changing it’s shape, or sculpting it’s form as they please. When this spell is directed at a being based on cold or ice, the caster can attempt to control it, unless the creature succeeds at an MR check. A creature controlled in this way is allowed to attempt a new check each day.":
        - cell "Control Cold"
        - cell "Water"

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Freeze Water 32 Inflicts intense cold on either a single individual or several people at once, freezing their bodies and enclosing them in ice prisons.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Crystallization Water 46 Transforms a body into a crystal, rendering it fragile and brittle. The target must succeed at an MR or PhR check to stop their whole body from freezing and being subject to an immediate state of Minor Paralysis. In addition, all damage received is automatically considered a Critical, regardless of the actual damage value. Creatures with Damage Resistance ignores the automatic critical effect, and instead treats their entire body as a vulnerable point.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Reflected Control Water 50 Mystically ties the victim’s body to the caster’s, forcing the target to mimic the caster’s every move.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Reflect States Water 56 The caster creates a mystic mirror, with the ability to reflect any supernatural state of which the caster, or any nearby individual, may be the target of. The effect automatically reflects upon the originator, who must succeed at an MR check to avoid falling victim to their own effect. For example, if the caster falls prey to a terrible magical pain, the spell would force the character causing the pain to succeed at an MR check or undergo the same suffering. The reflected state remains for as long as the original target continues to suffer the effects, or until this spell is dissipated. Each effect can only be reflected (or attempted to be reflected) once.":

        - cell "Water"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Ice Storm Water 60 Creates a heavy ice storm that freezes anything within it. Anyone within it’s range must succeed at a PhR check every 5 combat turns they remain within or automatically suffer 10 damage from the cold, and a -5 all action penalty, which accumulates with successive failures. Due to the extreme conditions and intense blizzards called forth by the storm, all perceptive ability checks have their difficulty increased by 2 levels. The storm has a radius from the origination point but remains stationary, and once a turn has passed from it’s creation, anyone inside must attempt the resistance check.":

        - cell "Water"

The spell has a maximum area of influence, which the caster may relocate after casting the spell.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Water Confinement Water 66 Produces a huge water mass that swallows all individuals it encounters. Whomever is trapped inside will move as if diving and will soon drown if unable to breathe in a liquid environment. Potent currents also push prisoners inside to the center of the prison, making it difficult to escape. Any attempt to do so requires succeeding at an opposed STR check, for which the spell uses a value of 14. Characters trapped inside can increase their effective Strength score by +1 for every level of difficulty above Easy they achieve with a Swim check. The water mass appears as a cube, whose area is determined by the level of the spell. All creatures inside the area of effect after the first round it is cast must attempt the opposed check.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Glacier Water 70 As implied by the name, this spell creates an immense glacial zone around the caster, totally independent of the prevailing weather conditions.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Tsunami Water 72 Raises a devastating Tsunami that ravages the coast for a given length. The eldritch tsunami destroys any construction with a damage barrier less than 80. Those above are still greatly damaged, but functional. All individuals along the area suffer the logical consequences.":
        - cell "Tsunami"
        - cell "Water"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Soul Reflection Water 76 Produces a spiritual reflection of a person or creature. The result is an animistic copy of the individual, featuring each and every quality present in the original. In game terms, the entity is identical to the original – except that it is a spiritual being. Although endowed with the capacity to interact with the real world, and visible to all (unless the original is also invisible), it is immune to attacks not capable of damaging energy. Powers of the original creature that require an elevated Gnosis above 20 cannot be duplicated. The reflection will obey the caster’s orders, but it can only materialize in the presence of the subject upon whose likeness it reflects. It can also only target beings within the level limit set by the level of the spell. Unwilling targets can attempt a MR check to avoid being copied, and the caster cannot target themselves.":
        - cell "Soul Reflection"
        - cell "Water"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Slow Time Water 80 Acting upon the fabric of space-time of a specific section of reality, time’s pace is slowed in such a way that it passes at a much slower rate for everyone within – including the caster. One minute in the area may equal one hour outside of it, or even longer, based on the level of the spell. The spell also has a final radius, but the zone initially only expands from the caster at a rate of 10 meters per combat turn. If someone enters it later, they are equally influenced. Only great and powerful entities, with a Gnosis value greater than 30, can avoid the consequences of the spell by succeeding at an MR check.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Freeze Magic Water 86 Freezes magic itself, temporarily stopping spells and canceling their effects. Spells do not dissipate while they are stopped. However, they are suspended and unable to affect anything, and they also do not require maintenance while in this state. Freeze magic affects spells both from the caster and from other characters, provided their combined Zeon is within the limit set by the level of the spell. The suspended spells will regain activity as soon as Freeze Magic dissipates. As this is a passive spell, it can even be used to stop spells activated within the same turn it is cast.":

        - cell "Water"

Only living beings with presence greater than 20, or locales with exceptional characteristics cannot be duplicated.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Dry Fire 46 Immediately dry any wet body or object within the designated radius. This effect can be used to harm elementals based on water. Any such being targeted must pass an MR or PhR check or suffer life point loss equal to double the level of failure. If used in hostility against living organisms that require water to survive (including humans), its effects can also be harmful, but damage is only half the level of failure for such creatures.":

        - cell "Fire"

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Melt Fire 50 Heats up any inorganic object the caster desires within it’s radius, causing it to reach extremely high temperatures.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Body of Fire Fire 52 Transforms a designated body into one of fire. Once transformed, a fiery body becomes immune to all attacks not based on cold, water, or energy. All methods of fire extinguishing (sand, foam, etc.) will injure the character in this state. Anyone who comes into contact with the flame body must pass a PhR check with a special difficulty equal to twice the caster’s presence. Failure damages the individual equal to half the level of failure, and every Heat AT the character has increases their resistance to this effect by +5.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Consume Essence Fire 62 Creates a terrifying supernatural aura around the caster that can destroy the vital essences of people, consuming their energy and preventing them from healing by natural means. Anyone in the radius of the spell must pass an MR check or suffer damage to both their Life Points and Zeon reserve equal to their level of failure. Life Points lost this way can not be recovered naturally, and must instead be restored by healing spells or other supernatural means. Zeon points lost in this way must be absorbed from other casters or from Zeonic containers.":

        - cell "Fire"

Life Points lost this way can not be recovered naturally, and must instead be restored by healing spells or other supernatural means. Zeon points lost in this way must be absorbed from other casters or from Zeonic containers."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Power Sacrifice Fire 66 Allows the caster to consume their own magical energy in order to increase their magical prowess. In game terms, the caster gains the passive ability to spend Zeon to temporarily increase their Magic Accumulation. For every 10 points of Zeon sacrificed for this purpose the caster gains +5 to their MA for the combat turn. This spell’s effects do not overlap, and only one Sacrifice spell may be active on an individual at a time.":

        - cell "Fire"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Direct Critical Fire 70 Causes an internal explosion within the target, causing a critical automatically. In game terms, the target receives the equivalent of a critical at a given level, although they have the ability to counter it with their PhR using the general rules. To avoid the effects altogether, the target can succeed at an MR check.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Magic for Capacities Fire 72 Allows the caster to consume their own magical reserve to increase their characteristics and abilities. In game terms, the caster gains the 39 passive ability to gain a +1 to one Characteristic for the current combat turn for every 25 Zeon sacrificed.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Fire Storm Fire 76 The caster selects an area where within a heavy fire storm appears, burning everything inside it to the ground.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Consume Life for Magic Fire 80 Allows the caster to consume their own vital energy in order to increase their magical reserves. In game terms, the spell grants the caster the passive ability temporarily sacrifice Life Points to recover Zeon. For every 5 life points sacrificed up to the limit per combat turn, the caster regains 100 points of Zeon. Damage resistant creatures sacrifice this number of life points multiplied by their damage resistance multiple. Life points lost this way recover at a rate of 10 per day, regardless of the character’s natural regeneration or magical healing they may employ (that is, it follows the rules for Sacrifice). Multiples of this spell do not overlap, and a character can only have one sacrifice spell active at a time.":

Life points lost this way recover at a rate of 10 per day, regardless of the character’s natural regeneration or magical healing they may employ (that is, it follows the rules for Sacrifice). Multiples of this spell do not overlap, and a character can only have one sacrifice spell active at a time."
    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Create Ifreet Fire 82 Creates a seemingly live creature completely under the magician’s control. This entity is developed as a Being Between Worlds, subject to the elemental powers and limitations of Fire Elementals described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings.     - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Sacrificing Others Fire 92 This spell binds other individuals’ essence and vital force to the caster, enabling them to be consumed for the caster’s advantage.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Armageddon Fire 100 The ultimate fire spell allows the caster to consume a portion of the world’s very essence, incinerating everything, even souls.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Detect Minerals Earth 2 The caster can detect a particular mineral’s location within the radius of the spell, provided it is not behind an energy barrier. This allows them to gather information about the location, relative size, and purity of the material, but not it’s shape.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Mineral Control Earth 6 Enables the caster to move, reshape, and control any mineral substance within the presence limit set by the level of the spell. However, the caster cannot endow the minerals with any capacities it does not already possess. In other words, the caster may reshape a pebble into an arrowhead, but cannot make it inflict electrical damage. Some golems and earth elementals can be controlled with this spell, unless they succeed at an MR check to resist. The check can only be repeated if the creature’s resistance values change, or if given an order contrary to it’s very nature.":
        - cell "Mineral Control"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "6"
        - cell "Enables the caster to move, reshape, and control any mineral substance within the presence limit set by the level of the spell.       - row "Firmness Earth 16 Enhances the endurance of individuals or objects, making them more resistant to physical damage.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Slowness Earth 22 Decreases the reaction speed and mobility of an individual. In game terms, if they do not succeed at an MR check, their initiative and movement value is decreased based on the level of the spell.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Shell Earth 26 Creates a physical shell which counts as armor, with it’s own AT value to all types of attack, except for Energy attacks. Even though this counts as an additional layer of armor, no initiative penalty is applied.":

        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "26"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Magnetic Shield Earth 30 By controlling magnetic fields around themselves, the caster raises a shield with the ability to repel any attack from a metallic source made against them, including bullets and arrows with metallic tips. In such cases, the shield’s magnetism causes a distortion that results in a -50 penalty to the attackers’ offensive abilities. The shield has a number of life points to absorb attacks, but is only able to be damaged by attacks capable of damaging energy. Naturally, this shield is practically useless against attacks of an immaterial or spiritual nature.":

        - cell "Earth"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Pass Through Solid Matter Earth 32 Allows the caster or those designated by them to pass through solid objects. The recipients of this spell do not exactly become immaterial, as they are still affected by the ambient heat or cold, but they can completely ignore any object not capable of stopping energy. In this way, characters can decide to pass through anything from walls to sword blades as if they did not exist. It is possible for the character to determine what materials they wish to pass through and what remains solid to them. This spell can affect a number of individuals as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell.":

        - cell "Earth"

This spell can affect a number of individuals as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Earth Spike Earth 36 Giant stone spikes erupt from the ground and impale targets upon the surface. This spell conjures a number of spikes with a Base Damage of 60, that use the Thrust attack type. These attacks can be used against a single target, or multiple, but cannot damage immaterial beings or those only capable of being damaged by energy, unless this attack is combined with an Enchant spell. Though this is an attack spell, it cannot be used to perform a spell clash, and it cannot be used against a target which at that moment is 10 meters or more from a solid surface.":

        - cell "Earth"

      - 'row "Forge Earth 50 Employs magic to forge and create objects, using the equivalent of a Forge ability determined by the level of the spell. Since this act of creation is based on supernatural means, no modifiers in Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Table 17: Time Employed applies. Additionally, there is no need for proper forging equipment.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Solid Body Earth 52 The targeted individual becomes immensely solid with stone-like resistance. When cast upon individuals, they receive natural armor which defends against all types of attacks (except for those of the Energy type), and a natural damage barrier equal to twice their Presence. As well, the character’s muscles strengthen, granting a bonus to their Strength score, but reducing their movement value by 2. The transformed individual appears to be made of different materials based on the level of the spell; Rock, Steel, Diamond, and Malebolgium.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Resistance Earth 56 Confers the temporary ability to absorb almost any damage inflicted on the individual. It provides a pool of additional Life Points, and the affected individual uses the rules for Damage Resistance, with a damage resistance multiple based on their size category. This prevents the character from using other defensive abilities just as a damage resistance creature. These bonus life points from this spell are subtracted before the character’s own when suffering any damage.":
        - cell "Resistance"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "56"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Petrify Earth 60 Transforms the target into a stone statue. The victim cannot move and has no awareness of the events going on around them for the duration of the spell. This can be maintained for years, but the affected character does not age. As soon as the spell dissipates, the individual returns to their original condition. Any damage or breakage to the statue can result in injury, or even death, to the actual character. The target of the spell can resist it’s effects by succeeding at an MR check. Upon failure, the subject is allowed a new check the following day for the first attempt, and then any subsequent attempt at weekly intervals.":
        - cell "Petrify"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "60"
        - cell "Transforms the target into a stone statue. The victim cannot move and has no awareness of the events going on around them for the duration of the spell. Any damage or breakage to the statue can result in injury, or even death, to the actual character. The target of the spell can resist it’s effects by succeeding at an MR check. Upon failure, the subject is allowed a new check the following day for the first attempt, and then any subsequent attempt at weekly intervals."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Fissure Earth 62 Causes a violent, but highly concentrated tremor that splits the earth, creating a fissure. The dimensions of the fissure’s maximum length and width are determined by the level of the spell, and can be between 20 to 50 meters deep. Individuals in the area of the fissure forming must pass an Agility check to avoid falling into the fissure and suffering the appropriate impact damage. Constructions in the area can also be severely damaged, as long as their damage barrier is sufficiently low. If they are above the limit set by the level of the spell, their structure is too dense or sturdy to be trapped or damaged by the fissure.":
        - cell "Fissure"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "62"
        - cell "Causes a violent, but highly concentrated tremor that splits the earth, creating a fissure. The dimensions of the fissure’s maximum length and width are determined by the level of the spell, and can be between 20 to 50 meters deep. Individuals in the area of the fissure forming must pass an Agility check to avoid falling into the fissure and suffering the appropriate impact damage. Constructions in the area can also be severely damaged, as long as their damage barrier is sufficiently low. If they are above the limit set by the level of the spell, their structure is too dense or sturdy to be trapped or damaged by the fissure."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Reverse Gravity Earth 66 Completely alters gravity in a particular area, reversing it’s force. In a way, it turns the world upside down. Everything within the area begins to ‘fall’ into the sky up to a maximum height set by the level of the spell. The caster may impose limits on this spell, such as only affecting the interior of a particular structure. Naturally, any object rooted or otherwise fixed to the ground does not fall. Individuals may avoid the effects entirely by succeeding at an MR check. The area affected remains stationary after being cast.":
        - cell "Reverse Gravity"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "66"
        - cell "Completely alters gravity in a particular area, reversing it’s force. In a way, it turns the world upside down. Everything within the area begins to ‘fall’ into the sky up to a maximum height set by the level of the spell. The caster may impose limits on this spell, such as only affecting the interior of a particular structure. Naturally, any object rooted or otherwise fixed to the ground does not fall. Individuals may avoid the effects entirely by succeeding at an MR check. The area affected remains stationary after being cast."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Mineral Creation Earth 70 Allows the caster to create anything they desire, provided it is composed of minerals or metals. The created object must be within the presence limit set by the level of the spell, and must appear in a logical location according to it’s form.":
        - cell "Mineral Creation"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "70"
        - cell "Allows the caster to create anything they desire, provided it is composed of minerals or metals. The created object must be within the presence limit set by the level of the spell, and must appear in a logical location according to it’s form."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Terrain Erudition Earth 72 The caster gains an immediate and total knowledge of everything in contact with the ground for a large area around them. Both constructions and living creatures can be detected straightaway, provided they are not immaterial in nature. The spell does not affect any places sealed with energy barriers.":
        - cell "Terrain Erudition"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "72"
        - cell "The caster gains an immediate and total knowledge of everything in contact with the ground for a large area around them. Both constructions and living creatures can be detected straightaway, provided they are not immaterial in nature. The spell does not affect any places sealed with energy barriers."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Earthquake Earth 76 Causes a devastating tremor in a large area with enough potential to destroy a city. Any construction with a Damage Barrier lower than 40 is immediately destroyed in the area, while those above 150 are not damaged at all. Those between 40 and 150 receive 5 points of damage in the first round, and is doubled every round thereafter (10 in the second, 20 in the third, etc.) All individuals in the area suffer the logical consequences for falling debris based on their surroundings.":
        - cell "Earthquake"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "76"
        - cell "Causes a devastating tremor in a large area with enough potential to destroy a city. Any construction with a Damage Barrier lower than 40 is immediately destroyed in the area, while those above 150 are not damaged at all. Those between 40 and 150 receive 5 points of damage in the first round, and is doubled every round thereafter (10 in the second, 20 in the third, etc.) All individuals in the area suffer the logical consequences for falling debris based on their surroundings."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Gravity Destruction Earth 80 Creates a high pressure gravity ‘bubble’ that can trap any physical entity and damage it to the point where it bursts. Trapped creatures must succeed at a PhR check of difficulty 180 each combat turn, or be subject to damage equal to half of the failure level. Characters receive a +5 bonus to this check for every point of Impact AT they possess. The extreme forces prevent the target from escaping the area of effect unless it succeeds at an opposed Strength check against the spell, which uses a value of 16. The power is so great that even immaterial beings are partially affected, though they apply a +40 bonus to the resistance roll, and +6 to their effective strength to escape. The spell affects everyone but the caster within it’s area, and is stationary once cast.":
        - cell "Gravity Destruction"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "80"
        - cell "Creates a high pressure gravity ‘bubble’ that can trap any physical entity and damage it to the point where it bursts. Trapped creatures must succeed at a PhR check of difficulty 180 each combat turn, or be subject to damage equal to half of the failure level. Characters receive a +5 bonus to this check for every point of Impact AT they possess. The extreme forces prevent the target from escaping the area of effect unless it succeeds at an opposed Strength check against the spell, which uses a value of 16. The power is so great that even immaterial beings are partially affected, though they apply a +40 bonus to the resistance roll, and +6 to their effective strength to escape. The spell affects everyone but the caster within it’s area, and is stationary once cast."
    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Create Golem Earth 82 Creates a seemingly live creature completely under the magician’s control. This entity is developed as a Being Between Worlds, subject to the elemental powers and limitations of Earth Elementals described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings. The creature has a number of development points based on the level of the spell, and the elemental is created using the same rules as the spell Create Being in the path of creation."':
        - cell "Create Golem"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "82"
        - 'cell "Creates a seemingly live creature completely under the magician’s control. This entity is developed as a Being Between Worlds, subject to the elemental powers and limitations of Earth Elementals described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy - Chapter 26: Creation of Beings. The creature has a number of development points based on the level of the spell, and the elemental is created using the same rules as the spell Create Being in the path of creation."'
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Gravity Increment Earth 86 Increases the mass of the atmosphere itself in a particular area, thus increasing the force of gravity, and therefore, the weight of everything inside. In game terms, the weight of all targets designated by the caster within the area of the spell increases. The spell’s location remains stationary after being cast.":
        - cell "Gravity Increment"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "86"
        - cell "Increases the mass of the atmosphere itself in a particular area, thus increasing the force of gravity, and therefore, the weight of everything inside. In game terms, the weight of all targets designated by the caster within the area of the spell increases. The spell’s location remains stationary after being cast."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Meteor Earth 90 Allows the caster to control the trajectory of one or more meteors and throw them from the heavens to cause unprecedented devastation. Despite being an attack spell, the arrival of each meteor requires between one and ten rounds after the cast to arrive, determined by rolling 1d10 to determine it’s arrival in combat turns after the spell is cast. Each meteor produces an attack with a base damage of 200 to the impact site and 10 meters around it, using either the Heat or Impact attack type. Everything within 50 meters of the central impact will suffer the effects of the blast wave, which is an attack with 60 base damage and an impact with a Strength score of 14. Naturally, the spell cannot produce it’s full effects if cast indoors or underground, so it’s consequences can be altered depending on the environment.":
        - cell "Meteor"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "90"
        - cell "Allows the caster to control the trajectory of one or more meteors and throw them from the heavens to cause unprecedented devastation. Despite being an attack spell, the arrival of each meteor requires between one and ten rounds after the cast to arrive, determined by rolling 1d10 to determine it’s arrival in combat turns after the spell is cast. Each meteor produces an attack with a base damage of 200 to the impact site and 10 meters around it, using either the Heat or Impact attack type. Everything within 50 meters of the central impact will suffer the effects of the blast wave, which is an attack with 60 base damage and an impact with a Strength score of 14. Naturally, the spell cannot produce it’s full effects if cast indoors or underground, so it’s consequences can be altered depending on the environment."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Gravity Control Earth 92 Grants the caster the ability to completely control all gravitational forces within the area. Within this zone, the caster will have absolute dominion over gravity, able to cancel it, increase or decrease it’s value up to 10 times normal strength, or reverse it. Every character in the area is automatically influenced by the chosen effects.":

        - cell "Earth"

Every character in the area is automatically influenced by the chosen effects."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "One with the Earth Earth 96 The caster merges their essence with the world, obtaining complete control over all minerals and metals in a large area. This allows them to alter the face of the earth by creating or removing mountains and valleys, redirecting rivers, and so forth. Every stone based creature within the spells area is required to succeed at an MR check in order to avoid being under the caster’s complete control. Upon failure, the creature can only repeat a check if their base resistance changes.":
        - cell "One with the Earth"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "96"
        - cell "The caster merges their essence with the world, obtaining complete control over all minerals and metals in a large area. This allows them to alter the face of the earth by creating or removing mountains and valleys, redirecting rivers, and so forth. Every stone based creature within the spells area is required to succeed at an MR check in order to avoid being under the caster’s complete control. Upon failure, the creature can only repeat a check if their base resistance changes."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Atomic Control Earth 100 The caster is able to mold the atomic matter of their surroundings like clay. To them, everything that moves is nothing but a set of atoms to manipulate as they desire. They gain full dominion over all organic and inorganic matter within the area. There is no limit to what the caster can do to with a body or object under their control; they may alter it’s shape and appearance, transform it into something else, or simply scatter its atoms, thereby causing it’s utter destruction. Since this spell controls physical matter, it has no effect on souls or completely immaterial bodies. Those wishing to avoid the effects must immediately succeed at an MR or PhR check, or they are forever subject to the control of the caster.":
        - cell "Atomic Control"
        - cell "Earth"
        - cell "100"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Natural Affinity Essence 2 Alters the essence of an individual, allowing them to bond with certain creatures. Such creatures recognize them as one of their own, for instance, the spell could be used to grant affinity with wolves, so that a pack would recognize the character as an actual wolf. The types of creatures affected depends on the level of the spell.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Detect Essence Essence 6 The caster is able to detect any being’s base essence in an area around them. The caster does not obtain any real information about the creature, but is able to recognize characteristics such as it’s elemental binding, and at a later time may recognize beings with the same essence or race. Creatures wishing to avoid the effects must succeed at an MR check. As this spell is not a Detection spell, the caster must be aware of the presence of someone nearby to read their essence, but it can affect spiritual and immaterial creatures.":

        - cell "Essence"

      - row "Communication Through Essence Essence 10 Establishes a connection between the caster and another living being, making it possible for them to communicate spiritually.       - row "Healing Essence 16 Instantly heals one character by restoring a percentage of their lost life points. This spell does not recover lost limbs, nor does it eliminate the other penalties of criticals, but it does stop any bleeding.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Soul Barrier Essence 20 Creates a barrier that stops all spiritual attacks affecting supernatural resistances. In game terms, it can stop attacks that force the defender to attempt an MR or PsR check of a certain difficulty or lower. It is not capable of stopping attacks that cause damage and also produce additional effects, such as Rain of Destruction. The shield itself does not have a life point value, as it does not stop attacks that produce damage.":

        - cell "Essence"

The shield itself does not have a life point value, as it does not stop attacks that produce damage."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Share Senses Essence 22 Allows the caster and other characters to share sight and hearing over a long distance. The caster may choose to deny access to their own senses. Characters can avoid being connected by succeeding at either an MR or PsR check, and are entitled to a new attempt every hour. The number of individuals affected besides the caster is limited only by their combined presence.":

        - cell "Essence"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Modify Essence Essence 26 Allows the caster to modify their own, or another character’s essence. This spell does not grant additional or special abilities as part of the change, but does temporarily alter the animistic base of the being. In game terms, this spell could turn an earth elemental into one of cold, causing it to be vulnerable to heat based effects and attacks. If the creature does possess special powers according to the aspect being modified, it temporarily loses those powers. To avoid this effect it is necessary to succeed at an MR check, which is only repeatable if the being’s base resistance changes.":

        - cell "Essence"

If the creature does possess special powers according to the aspect being modified, it temporarily loses those powers. To avoid this effect it is necessary to succeed at an MR check, which is only repeatable if the being’s base resistance changes."
    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Soul Poison Essence 30 Soul poison is a supernatural substance, inoculated into the target and spreading throughout their body. The caster is free to determine the effects of the poison, following the guidelines in Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Chapter 14: States and Accidents. The level of the poison is determined by the level of the spell. Additionally, this spell can be used to create antidotes should enough information to deduce one be available."':

        - cell "Essence"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Analyze Soul Essence 32 Allows the caster to spiritually analyze an individual or creature, obtaining detailed information about their natural capacities and powers. Although it does not confer access to the victim’s knowledge, or the level of the abilities that depend on it, the caster can measure the victim’s spiritual potential and their innate capabilities. For example, when directed at a supernatural being, the caster can discover their powers and level, but not what the creature knows, or how good of a fighter it is. To avoid these effects, the target must succeed at an MR check.":

        - cell "Essence"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Alter Growth Essence 46 Modifies the natural rhythm of an organism's development, accelerating or delaying its evolution. In game terms, it allows multiplying or dividing the aging rate of a living body based on the level of the spell. For example, if this spell is cast at the advanced degree to accelerate someone's aging, each day that passes will be equivalent to fifty for the target. To resist the spell’s effects, the target must succeed at an MR check. Once someone is affected by this spell, he has no right to repeat the resistance check as long as the caster maintains the spell.":

        - cell "Essence"

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Natural Imitation Essence 50 Creates one or more natural animals, imitating existing species. The beings will be under the absolute control of the caster, who can give them orders through the mystical bond that unites them. The created creatures must be an animal with Gnosis 0 and a race that exists in the world (for example, bears, rabbits, elephants ...). The spell allows you to create a single being (Level 5 limit), or distribute the levels it confers among several creatures. Creations will have all their natural instincts and abilities. As a reference, the caster should use the animals of Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Chapter 25: Common Characters for reference."':

        - cell "Essence"
        - cell "50"
        - 'cell "Creates one or more natural animals, imitating existing species.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Natural Control Essence 56 The caster gains absolute dominion over a single living being with Gnosis 0. Valid targets include plants, animals, and even regular human beings. The caster transmits their orders directly through a mystic connection, without need for language. Resisting the effects of this spell requires a successful MR check. A character cannot substitute their Psychic resistance, since the effect is control through the target’s essence, and not their mind. The victim is allowed a new resistance check every day, as well as any time it receives an order against it’s nature.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Return to the Flow Essence 62 Sends a spirit back to the flow of souls. This spell can affect both a dead soul of a normal creature awaiting the call, and a creature of the Spirit category. The essence vanishes once it arrives to the great beyond unless the entity has a Gnosis level high enough to simply return to the real world.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Greenness Essence 90 By merging their spirit with nature itself, the caster is able to create plant and animal life as they please within a large radius. Such life emerges instantly, regardless of local climate and terrain conditions. However, since the magical energy of the spell has no duration, the extended survival of the newly created life is not guaranteed. Animals and plants created with this spell must belong to natural existing species.":
        - cell "Greenness"
        - cell "Essence"
        - cell "90"
        - cell "By merging their spirit with nature itself, the caster is able to create plant and animal life as they please within a large radius. Such life emerges instantly, regardless of local climate and terrain conditions. However, since the magical energy of the spell has no duration, the extended survival of the newly created life is not guaranteed. Animals and plants created with this spell must belong to natural existing species."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Life Dominion Essence 92 By seizing control of a portion of the world’s very essence, the caster gains absolute dominion over all Natural beings in a large area. They transmit their orders to such creatures through a mystical connection, without the need for language or physical proximity. Any entity wishing to resist the effects of this spell must succeed at an MR check. Any affected being is entitled to a new check every day as well as any time they receive an order completely against their nature.":
        - cell "Life Dominion"
        - cell "Essence"
        - cell "92"
        - cell "By seizing control of a portion of the world’s very essence, the caster gains absolute dominion over all Natural beings in a large area. They transmit their orders to such creatures through a mystical connection, without the need for language or physical proximity. Any entity wishing to resist the effects of this spell must succeed at an MR check. Any affected being is entitled to a new check every day as well as any time they receive an order completely against their nature."
    - rowgroup:
      - row:
        - cell "Resurrection"
        - cell "Essence"
        - cell "96"
        - cell
    - rowgroup:
      - row:
        - cell "Lord of Souls"
        - cell "Essence"
        - cell "100"
        - cell "The caster seizes control of a portion of the flow of souls itself, giving him absolute dominion over all souls, Spirits, and similar beings in a wide radius. Among other uses, the caster can snatch a soul from one body and reintroduce it into an unborn child, thus producing Nephalim. The lord of souls conveys their orders to those under their dominion through a mystical connection that does not require physical proximity nor language. Those wishing to resist these effects must succeed at an MR check, and those who fail this check are entitled to a new check each day, or any time they are given an order completely against their nature. If the caster is attempting to separate the soul from it’s original body, the target receives a special +40 to their resistance roll. Naturally, this spell only affects creatures with souls, and so it does not affect the undead or inanimate constructs."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Illusory Sound Illusion 2 Allows the caster to create any sound, including human voices, in an area around them. All subjects within the area must pass an MR check to disbelieve the effect, though the caster may choose those within the area who hear the illusion.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Sweet Talk Illusion 20 Enhances a person’s charisma and personal charm. In game terms, the character affected receives a bonus to their Leadership and Persuasion secondary abilities.":

        - cell "Illusion"
        - cell "20"
        - cell "Enhances a person’s charisma and personal charm. In game terms, the character affected receives a bonus to their Leadership and Persuasion secondary abilities."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Alter Appearance Illusion 22 Allows the caster to change the appearance of an individual or object into that of another of their choice. This spell only allows an increase or decrease of one’s size by two degrees. All subjects in contact with the image can attempt to disbelieve with an MR check. Once failed, a new check is only allowed when they have reason to doubt the identity or appearance of the affected target.":

      - row "Illusory Invisibility Illusion 26 Allows the caster to make any being or object vanish from sight, as long as their combined presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell.       - row "Mirror Image Illusion 30 Creates a number of copies of a specific target. These are only illusory images, and cannot be separated by more than 10 meters from each other. Discerning the copies from the original requires a successful MR check.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Total Illusion Illusion 32 Creates a complete illusion that can deceive all five of a victim’s natural senses. The caster can create anything inanimate they desire. The illusion is destroyed if damaged by an attack capable of damaging energy.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Confusion Illusion 36 Confounds all five natural senses of a single target. The victim must succeed at an MR check or suffer a penalty equal to their failure level to all perception based secondary abilities. Furthermore, if failed by a margin of 40 or greater, they suffer an All Action Penalty of -20 due to dizziness. The target is not entitled to a new resistance check unless they increase their base resistance value.":
