    - rowgroup:
      - row "Detect Lie Illusion 46 Automatically detects any lie told in the caster’s presence. Every time a lie is deliberately told before them, the liar must succeed at either an MR or PsR check to prevent the caster from finding out. Lies unknowingly told are unaffected by this spell.":

        - cell "46"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Ghostly Illusion Illusion 50 Creates one or more objects, which are governed by the rules for a Ghostly spell. Any inanimate object the caster desires can be produced, from a sword to a wall, provided the theoretical presence of the object is within the limit set by the level of the spell. A character or creature can avoid the spell and ignore the illusion if they succeed at an MR check. The check is only allowed to be repeated if the character has a reason to doubt the reality of the object.":
        - cell "Ghostly Illusion"
        - cell "Illusion"
        - cell "50"
        - cell "Creates one or more objects, which are governed by the rules for a Ghostly spell. Any inanimate object the caster desires can be produced, from a sword to a wall, provided the theoretical presence of the object is within the limit set by the level of the spell. A character or creature can avoid the spell and ignore the illusion if they succeed at an MR check. The check is only allowed to be repeated if the character has a reason to doubt the reality of the object."
    - rowgroup:
      - row:
        - cell "Distort Detection"
        - cell "Illusion"
        - cell "52"
        - cell "Grants the caster the ability to alter the result of any supernatural detection around them. They can falsify the information in any way they desire, increasing or decreasing it’s potential, its abilities, or location of detection. The character that casts the detection ability must succeed at an MR check to avoid being deceived. The spell does not work directly against individuals within the area of effect, but against the detection method itself. Therefore, if someone tries to detect something that is within the radius of the spell, they must also succeed at the MR check or be affected. It would, for example, affect a sorcerer who from miles away tries to magically locate the caster or someone next to them. For this same reason, by directly affecting the means of detection, the person who uses it does not realize that they had to overcome the MR check to avoid being deceived."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Lie Illusion 56 Allows the caster to convince their audience to believe anything they say, no matter how ridiculous or absurd. The affected parties will not necessarily obey orders from them, but they will believe what is said as true. Any victim may avoid the results of this spell by succeeding at an MR check, which is able to be reattempted each hour. In cases of exceptionally unbelievable lies, or if the victims are warned of this ability, they may apply a +40 to their resistance.":
        - cell "Lie"
        - cell "Illusion"
        - cell "56"
        - cell "Allows the caster to convince their audience to believe anything they say, no matter how ridiculous or absurd. The affected parties will not necessarily obey orders from them, but they will believe what is said as true. Any victim may avoid the results of this spell by succeeding at an MR check, which is able to be reattempted each hour. In cases of exceptionally unbelievable lies, or if the victims are warned of this ability, they may apply a +40 to their resistance."
    - rowgroup:

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Ghostly Being Illusion 62 Creates a ghostly being. The entity is fashioned according to the caster’s desires, using the rules described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Chapter 26: Creation of Beings. This spell functions for all intents and purposes like the spell Illusory Being from this path, except for that whomever fails the MR check is subject to the rules for Ghostly spells. It is possible to perform a new resistance roll only if a character has reason to disbelieve the reality of the entity. The being can be a maximum of 3 levels above the caster."':
        - cell "Ghostly Being"
        - cell "Illusion"
        - cell "62"

It is possible to perform a new resistance roll only if a character has reason to disbelieve the reality of the entity. The being can be a maximum of 3 levels above the caster."'

    - rowgroup:
      - row "The Gift of Lying Illusion 72 The caster gains the ability to force their victim to lie. The lying behavior can be about anything the victim says, or restricted to a certain subject. This can be avoided with a successful MR or PsR check, which can only be repeated once a day upon failure.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Illusory Life Illusion 76 Grants the caster the ability to introduce false memories into the mind of an individual. The caster can alter their memories, including any and all new data they desire. Any person influenced by this spell is not able to differentiate illusory memories from their own.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Major Illusion Illusion 80 Creates an enormous illusion able to affect all five natural senses. The spell develops over a large area, within which anyone can see, hear, feel, smell, and taste whatever the caster desires. For instance, the caster may trick someone into thinking a whole town is deserted, while in actuality it is a thriving city. Any character entering the area affected is automatically affected unless they pass an MR check.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Fix Illusion Illusion 82 Creates a ‘permanent’ illusion. In game terms, it adds a number of Zeon points to be used for maintenance of any spell of the path of illusion. Bear in mind this amount will not serve to increase the degree at which the spell is cast, and only provides the ability to maintain the illusion constantly. This cannot be used to maintain any free access spell, even if the ‘slot’ used is within the path of illusion.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row:
        - cell "Illusion of the Senses"
        - cell "Illusion"
        - cell "86"
        - cell "Allows the caster to lead a subject to believe whatever the caster wishes. Though the caster does not actually modify reality, the spell convinces the affected character that an illusion is so real, they will act as if it is so in every aspect. For example, if the caster wishes to trick someone into thinking they have lost all of their arms and legs, the character will lose all feeling in those limbs and fall to the ground in the belief that they have in fact, lost them. Another example of this could be a fighter suffering from wounds who is led to believe that not only are they in perfect shape, but also that they possess a +50 all action bonus (which is of course, fictional). A character convinced they are dead falls immediately into unconsciousness. If the associated MR or PsR is failed, they receive a new check only upon being convinced they are being deceived."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Non-Existence Illusion 90 The target of this spell cannot be perceived by any of the natural senses – although they still materially exist. Others cannot see, hear, smell, touch, or taste the presence of the individual in any way. In addition, the target does not leave any visible trace or marks behind while the spell is maintained. The only way to realize a non-existent character’s presence is via supernatural means (such as magic, ki, or psychic detections). A character can resist the effects of the spell by passing an MR check. Those who fail are entitled to repeat it every time they have reason to believe there is another being around.":
        - cell "Non-Existence"
        - cell "Illusion"
        - cell "90"
        - cell "The target of this spell cannot be perceived by any of the natural senses – although they still materially exist. Others cannot see, hear, smell, touch, or taste the presence of the individual in any way. In addition, the target does not leave any visible trace or marks behind while the spell is maintained. The only way to realize a non-existent character’s presence is via supernatural means (such as magic, ki, or psychic detections). A character can resist the effects of the spell by passing an MR check. Those who fail are entitled to repeat it every time they have reason to believe there is another being around."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Deceive Death Illusion 92 The caster manages to deceive death itself. In game terms, while active, the individual will not die in any way, as the flow of souls ignores their presence and will not claim them. Although the spirit will not leave the body, the physical form can be shattered, and the character continues to suffer the negative effects of such damage. This spell only affects characters within a certain level limit. This spell does not protect from an effect that completely destroys the soul of the individual.":
        - cell "Deceive Death"
        - cell "Illusion"
        - cell "92"

This spell only affects characters within a certain level limit. This spell does not protect from an effect that completely destroys the soul of the individual."

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Drain Life Necromancy 12 Allows the caster to absorb the life force of another living creature, severely injuring it in the process. The caster must be able to physically touch the target, who must succeed at an MR check or lose a number of life points equal to the level of failure. The caster then adds this amount of life points to their own, however they cannot exceed their normal maximum. Creatures with damage resistance lose 5 times the normal amount, but the caster only restores the normal amount to their own life points. For example, if a damage resistant creature fails by 20 points, they would lose 100 LP, but the caster would only restore 20LP.":

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Raise Corpses Necromancy 28 By manipulating necrotic energy, the caster can animate corpses and turn them into zombies or skeletons under their control. The dead do not keep any special abilities or knowledge from their life, as they are merely shells of their former selves. However, they retain natural capabilities, such as natural weapons and some physical characteristics. The power and endurance of the creature varies depending on its body, for example the corpse of a bear is considerably more dangerous than one of a human. Basic information on zombies can be referenced in Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Chapter 25: Common Characters. The caster can raise a number of corpses, as long as their combined presence and individual levels are within the limits set by the spell."':

        - cell "Necromancy"

The caster can raise a number of corpses, as long as their combined presence and individual levels are within the limits set by the spell."'
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Dead Body Necromancy 30 Temporarily stops the bodily functions of an individual without killing them, although they are still able to move and act completely normally. As long as the spell remains active, the character will be extremely resistant to the effects of damage and its consequences, so any physical penalty is halved. Additionally, in the state between life and death, it allows the character to remain conscious and ignore penalties (except those caused by criticals). Anyone who inspects the body will be unable to discover that it is not a true corpse. Affects any character up to the level determined by the spell.":

        - cell "Necromancy"

      - row "Destroy Undead Necromancy 36 Completely destroys the essence of an undead target. Creatures with damage resistance multiply this amount by their damage resistance multiple.":

        - cell "Necromancy"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Drain Characteristics Necromancy 38 By touching a living creature, the caster can absorb one of the individual’s characteristics, increasing their own in the process. The caster must decide the characteristic being drained before executing the spell. The target loses 1 point from this characteristic for every 10 points by which they fail the associated MR check. If the natural value of the characteristic drained is higher than the caster’s, the drained points are absorbed at a one to one ratio. However if the caster has a higher natural value in this characteristic, they must drain 3 points to absorb 1 to their own. These changes last as long as the spell is maintained, and once dismissed the characteristics recover at a rate of 1 point per hour.":
        - cell "Drain Characteristics"
        - cell "Necromancy"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Control The Dead Necromancy 40 The caster obtains absolute control over any undead creature in the area when cast. Dominion over the creatures remains active as long as the spell is maintained, but does not affect any other necromantic creatures that approach later. The creatures can avoid these effects by passing a MR check, and are only allowed another attempt when given an order completely contrary to their nature. As such, creatures without will, such as animated corpses, can never be freed.":

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Necromantic Modification Necromancy 56 The undead creature that is the target of this spell feeds upon it’s energy, temporarily gaining new powers and abilities.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Summon The Dead Necromancy 58 Calls the souls of the dead or specters to the caster. They are able to summon these spirits selectively, although it is possible some other soul may appear in it’s place. Summon the Dead only works upon spirits still awaiting the call, or spectral creatures, with a maximum level based on the level of the spell. It does not affect souls already transmigrated to the Flow of Souls, or those housed in new bodies.":

        - cell "Necromancy"

It does not affect souls already transmigrated to the Flow of Souls, or those housed in new bodies."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Raise Specters Necromancy 60 By dominating the souls of the dead, the caster can pervert their essences and turn them into specters in their service. The ghosts keep a small part of the capacities they used to have in life, but lose the majority of their old powers and attributes. A specter raised by this spell reduces to half (rounded up) their level achieved in life. Although it does use the general rule for spiritual creatures, it obtains an additional 100 Development Points to acquire powers deemed appropriate by the caster with Gnosis 20. This spell only works on newly deceased souls who still await the call to the Flow of Souls. It can affect several spirits at the same time, as long as the sum of their presences are within the limit set by the spell.":

        - cell "Necromancy"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Drain Life Force Necromancy 62 Absorbs the life force of an individual and transmits it to whomever is designated by the caster. The character that is to be drained must succeed at an MR check, or lose a point of both Constitution and Power for every 10 points of failure. If the caster wishes, the target may also be aged by this process, by a number of years equal to the level of failure. The absorbed points allow the caster or the subject who receives them to rejuvenate and recover their lost Constitution and Power, up to their natural levels. Characters drained of their vital essence cannot recover it, except through supernatural abilities that allow it (like this one).":

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Necromantic Chimera Necromancy 70 Creates an undead creature subject to the caster’s control. This entity is developed as a Being Between worlds and Gnosis 25 using the rules described in Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Chapter 26: Creation of Beings. However, the caster cannot create the entity from scratch; in order to develop it, they must gather it’s pieces from several corpses. If looking to endow any power or essential ability, it must find the bodies of those who possessed those powers in life. For example, if wanting to create a creature with natural flight, they must find the corpse of a creature who had that specific ability when alive. The existence of the chimera is linked to the caster’s soul, and as such is subject to the same maximum level rules as the spell Create Being in the path of creation."':
        - cell "Necromantic Chimera"
        - cell "Necromancy"
        - cell "70"
        - 'cell "Creates an undead creature subject to the caster’s control. However, the caster cannot create the entity from scratch; in order to develop it, they must gather it’s pieces from several corpses. If looking to endow any power or essential ability, it must find the bodies of those who possessed those powers in life. For example, if wanting to create a creature with natural flight, they must find the corpse of a creature who had that specific ability when alive. The existence of the chimera is linked to the caster’s soul, and as such is subject to the same maximum level rules as the spell Create Being in the path of creation."'

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Vassalage Necromancy 76 Permanently ties an undead creature’s soul to that of the caster. Thus, the caster automatically becomes it’s hierarchical superior, and the creature’s existence is now fully dependent on the caster. Although this spell does not endow the necromancer with real control over the creature, their essence is tied together such that if the master is destroyed, the creature perishes as well. The affected undead may avoid these effects by succeeding at an MR or PhR check.":

        - cell "Necromancy"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Drain Souls Necromancy 78 The caster plucks the soul of another person, feeding on their essence and power. The stronger the spirit absorbed, the greater energy received. The individual targeted by this spell must succeed at an MR check, or lose an amount of presence equal to half the level of failure. Every five points lost also subtracts a level, and with it any capacity or power that depended upon it. If the target’s presence reaches 0, their soul is completely consumed and they die. For every 10 points of presence absorbed, the caster temporarily increases one of their characteristics by 1, or obtains 10 Development Points to obtain any essential ability or power of a creature with Gnosis 30. Every day, the caster loses 1 of these temporary characteristics, or 20 of the increased Development Points.":

        - cell "Necromancy"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "True Rise Necromancy 82 Raises a corpse allowing it to maintain it’s knowledge and every one of it’s powers and abilities it had in life. Unfortunately, the raised creature becomes an undead that lacks a true soul. The spell affects deceased creatures or characters with a maximum level based on the level of the spell, and whose bodies are within reach of the caster. If death occurred a long time before the spell is cast, the raised creature’s physical abilities might be damaged. Characters resurrected once through this spell can not be resurrected again. As well, even though it is an undead entity, it retains it’s free will and is not under the caster’s control.":

      - row "Cursed Land Necromancy 88 By corrupting a small fragment of the world, the caster enchants an area of land, causing any being that dies within to rise instantly as an undead under their control.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Come Back From The Dead Necromancy 98 Returns a deceased creature’s spirit to the world, even after it has returned to the flow of souls or if their essence has been dispersed.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Aura of Chaos Chaos 14 The wizard causes a surge of energy that alters fate and makes events chaotic and unpredictable. Any situation the target is involved in whose result is determined by chance will always have improbable results, whether for good or ill. A card player, for example, would continuously have either incredibly lucky or unlucky streaks. Characters with the Good Luck Advantage or Bad Luck Disadvantage suffer exponentially increased effects. It is possible to avoid these effects by passing a MR check determined by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Paths of Chaos Chaos 44 The wizard can reroll a certain number of rolls per turn, provided that none of them are a Fumble. It is not possible to reroll the same roll twice.":

        - cell "Chaos"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Manipulate Chaos Chaos 64 Through the power of chaos, this spell can change a person’s action, possibly changing its outcome. The spell must be cast immediately after someone performs an action, and the character who took that action must immediately reroll. For example, a character who attempts to jump across a gap and fails can try again. He is “rewound” to the beginning of the jump and must attempt the jump again with a new roll unless he passes a MR check. This spell takes effect automatically and can affect anyone in the vicinity of the wizard at the moment the target completes an action. Creatures with Gnosis 35 or higher or Natura +20 are aware of the change, while others cannot perceive it.":
        - cell "Manipulate Chaos"
        - cell "Chaos"
        - cell "64"

This spell takes effect automatically and can affect anyone in the vicinity of the wizard at the moment the target completes an action. Creatures with Gnosis 35 or higher or Natura +20 are aware of the change, while others cannot perceive it."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Chaotic Mutation Chaos 74 The target of this spell suffers serious mutations in his body, altering both his physiognomy and his powers. The wizard or individual targeted with Chaotic Mutation gets additional Development Points to spend on powers like a Being Between Worlds with Gnosis 25. However, he can only voluntarily choose where to spend half of those Development Points; the other half are determined randomly by the Game Master. Almost always, the power of this spell leaves visible physical changes. A person can pass a MR check at the difficulty set by the level of the spell to resist its effects. The new Development Points increase the target’s level as normal. This spell can only be cast on an individual once.":
        - cell "Chaotic Mutation"
        - cell "Chaos"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Primal Chaos Chaos 94 Completely altering the laws of reality, the wizard creates chaos in its purest form, an area where everything stops responding to any laws of reason. Space is distorted; a road leading a character up may actually take him downwards; miles are traveled in seconds and feet in hours. A person can be young and old at the same time, a storm may pummel the area on a sunny day… Nothing makes sense, because the concept of logic has ceased to exist. A normal person will likely go crazy in a few minutes, while all supernatural power may go out of control. The area covered by this spell is determined by its level, is stationary, and is centered on where it was cast.":
        - cell "Primal Chaos"
        - cell "Chaos"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Written Order Literature 34 This spell allows the wizard to write a simple command on a scroll that will affect the first person to read it, forcing him to comply if he fails to resist the spell. The command will work even if it is hidden among other phrases, although the affected person can get between a +20 and a +60 bonus to his Resistance check if the command goes against his nature. This spell can be repeated several times and chained to create a complex order, such as “Open the door” then “Forget it happened.” The words lose power after the spell affects a person or is resisted by him, and the command is treated as ordinary writing from then on.":
        - cell "Written Order"
        - cell "Literature"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Theater of Life Literature 54 This spell allows the wizard to modify the behavior of people around him by writing on a surface the actions he wants them to take. Basically, the wizard becomes the writer of a play, turning people into actors who perform their tasks as he dictates. This spell can affect many people, provided their combined Presences do not exceed the limit set by the level of the spell. For someone to be affected, he must be within the area of the spell and the caster must know his true name, though everyone affected may pass a MR or PsR check to avoid the spell’s effects. If the narrator stops writing and those affected have no new orders to follow, they will stay still, waiting for their next roles.":
        - cell "Theater of Life"
        - cell "Literature"

    - rowgroup:

To avoid the effects of Tragedy, either the target or anyone else involved in the target’s fate must overcome the spell’s MR.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Anima Music 94 This spell carries a tune of supernatural power which penetrates the soul of anyone who hears it. Anima must be cast in combination with a Spiritual spell, and contrary to the usual rules, both spells are cast together in one action by the wizard (without a penalty for doing so). The wizard must pay the initial Zeon costs and the maintenance costs of both spells as normal. However, instead of the Spiritual spell taking effect immediately, it becomes Automatic and affects everyone within 150 feet of the caster who hears it for at least one full turn. The MR check to overcome the Spiritual spell is the same as for the normal spell, but if someone partially covers his ears, he gets a +40 bonus to his check.":

        - cell "Music"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Pleasant Conversation Nobility 44 This spell allows the caster to perceive the surface thoughts and major interests of his target, making it easier for him to engage the target in a pleasant and enjoyable conversation. The victim may resist by making a MR or PsR check. If the target fails, the caster gets a bonus of +200 to all Style and Persuasion rolls intended to fascinate him and pull him into a casual conversation. For the spell to work, the caster must talk for at least half a minute to a target who is not openly hostile. Although there is no maintenance cost, the spell only lasts until the conversation stops.":

        - cell "Nobility"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Grandeur Nobility 54 This spell imbues the wizard with an air of grandeur, giving him great authority over those accustomed to obeying orders. Anyone within or entering a 15 foot radius area around him must pass a MR or PsR check or be forced to please him, treating him with the respect and deference due to a superior. A soldier will not ask him questions, an innkeeper will offer his best table, and servants will kneel to him and quickly respond to his slightest desire. Grandeur has no effect on individuals who have a marked hostility against the caster, who know and feel they are his equals or superiors, or those beings who simply believe that they could not obey the caster under any circumstances. Targets may repeat the check only if the caster makes an action so contrary to reason and his supposed status that they are forced to reconsider their obedience to him.":
        - cell "Grandeur"
        - cell "Nobility"
        - cell "54"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Win Hearts Nobility 64 This spell alters people’s perceptions to make them feel that the caster or his chosen target is a being of beauty and intoxicating sensuality. In addition, the target gets a bonus of +200 to Persuasion (Seduction) against anyone who has failed this check. In order to cast this spell on someone else, the caster must first study the appearance of the person to benefit from this spell in depth.":

      - 'row "Shield of Salvation Peace 4 This spell creates a protective barrier that protects against all kinds of attacks.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Detect Sin Sin 4 This spell allows the caster to see the sins the target has committed. He will not get specific information about them (he will know generally what type of sin the target committed but not specifically what it was), but can feel how severe it was and approximately how long ago it happened. For example, he might know that a person has lied recently, but not where or why he lied. The caster will detect the greatest sins first, starting with the most recent. Multiple sins of the same type can be detected. Casting the spell on the same target a second time will provide the same sins (unless the Resistance roll is different, in which case more or less can be revealed, or unless the target has committed new serious sins).":

        - cell "Sin"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Imbue with Sin Sin 14 The target of the spell is compelled to commit the cardinal sin that he is the most closely associated with. The target is only affected if he fails a MR check, and he can check again to end the spell every time he is confronted with something that might cause him to sin in this way — although even if he passes, his normal impulses may still drive him to sin.":

      - row "Gluttony Sin 24 This spell allows the wizard to automatically gain a portion of all the Zeon points (rounded up to the nearest 5) and Ki points spent in the spell’s radius around him. Gluttony has no effect on Innate spells or magical powers that do not consume Zeon or Ki, and it also does not affect the caster’s own spells or Ki Abilities.":

        - cell "4"
        - cell "This spell grants the caster knowledge about the nature of an object. Target objects can range from a plate of food, which would give him knowledge of the recipes used to make the meal, to a complex device, which would grant him a rudimentary understanding of how the device was built and what materials were used. This spell does not give the caster the knowledge of how to build the object, but at least guides him in the direction of the needed components, materials, or tools. Objects or buildings that are particularly complex or that have a very high Presence are immune to this spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Knowledge Knowledge 14 This spell gives the caster innate knowledge regarding a chosen subject. He gains a new value in the knowledge-related Secondary Ability he chooses, replacing his base value. He forgets the knowledge and reverts to his base value when he stops maintaining the spell. A character may cast several copies of this spell and maintain them simultaneously, but must choose a different ability each time.":

This spell can uncover vulnerable points in creatures and objects. The target must be in the vicinity of the caster, and if the target wishes to resist the spell’s effects, the target must pass a MR check with a difficulty determined by the level of the spell.":

Once Magical Knowledge has been cast, the wizard has a maximum of five turns to cast the new spell. After five turns or when the new spell is cast, it disappears from his mind.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Transfusion Blood 44 This spell separates some of the vital energy from the caster’s blood and transfers it to an ally, effectively transferring some of the caster’s Life Points. The amount of Life Points the caster sacrifices is multiplied by an amount determined by the level of the spell. For example, if a wizard casts Transfusion at the Intermediate level and sacrifices 10 Life Points, his chosen ally will recover 50 Life Points. This spell does not regenerate severed limbs or other permanent losses, but it does reduce penalties caused by Criticals by an amount equal to the Life Points the caster sacrificed. The Life Points the caster gives up recover at the rate of a Sacrifice (i.e., 10 LP per day).":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Exsanguinate Blood 54 This spell causes any wounds the target sustains to bleed horribly, greatly increasing the damage he suffers from attacks. While this spell is active, whenever the target is struck by an attack that deals Impact, Cut, or Thrust damage, he suffers additional damage as determined by the level of the spell. Exsanguinate will not amplify damage caused by the Heat, Cold, Electricity, or Energy Attack Types. To avoid this spell’s effects, the target must pass a MR check against the spell when it is first cast on him. Whenever the target suffers damage that has been amplified by the spell, he may repeat the MR check to end the spell’s effects after the damage has been suffered.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Night Walker Dreams 44 This spell allows the wizard or a person chosen by him to bring his dream self into the real world. Thus, while his body sleeps, he can move freely around the world as a dream form. While someone is in this dream form, he is invisible to the naked eye (except to those capable of seeing spirits) and makes no noise. He is also completely intangible and cannot touch anything material. The maximum distance that the person can move from his physical body is determined by the level of the spell. If the dream form or physical body sustains any damage, the spell is broken.":

The caster can choose whether he sends the whole target or only his mind (i.e., the target’s body will sleep while his mind goes to the Wake). If the target was already asleep, he cannot feel the dimensional jump, and if he was awake, his body falls into a deep sleep. The spell can also affect creatures native to the Wake, which it destroys completely. To resist this spell, the target must pass a MR check with a difficulty determined by the level of the spell.":

    - rowgroup:

Creatures that have special powers in the Wake continue to be able to use them in the area affected.":

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Make Dreams Real Dreams 94 This spell does literally what its name implies: it allows the wizard to bring into the real world any being or object that is in a sleeper’s dreams. For example, it could bring a shield, a warrior, or a horse that the dreamer rode into reality. In doing so, the object actually materializes and gains “substance.” In the case of a living being, the creature is apparently real, but cannot have powers greater than those allowed to a creature with Gnosis 25 or have a class level above that allowed by the level of the spell. In the case of an object, its maximum Presence is determined by the level of the spell. It is important to note that this spell does not allow the caster to see what is actually inside another’s dreams, so it will be necessary to use some other means to “see” exactly what he wants to make real."':

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Stop Time Time 44 This spell makes time stand still inside a given space. Beginning with the turn after the spell is cast, every individual or thing in the area of effect is frozen in time if it fails an MR check. Persons or things frozen in time are completely impervious; nothing can interact with them, touch them, or damage them, and they cannot interact with anything else in any way. Creatures with Gnosis 40 or more can ignore this effect and interact freely with frozen things. Anyone affected by this spell can only repeat the MR check once a day. The area affected by Stop Time remains at the location it was cast, and the caster cannot choose specific targets; everyone is affected equally except for the caster.":

        - cell "Time"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Rewind Time 54 This spell affects the essence of a person, moving his “life” backwards in time. Naturally, the person concerned does not travel to the past himself; his essence merely reverts to the way it was at an earlier point in time. Thus, an elder could become a young man, or an adult could become a mere child (or even a baby). Unfortunately, the spell also removes the character’s memories, so he loses that part of his life, forgetting his memories or losing (or gaining) capabilities that changed in the lost years. Naturally, this essence reversion can also change the affected person’s level. This spell cannot restore severed limbs, end effects, or undo damage caused by beings with Gnosis 40 or higher. To resist the spell’s effects, the target must pass a MR check with a difficulty determined by the level of the spell.":

        - cell "Time"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Time Jump Time 74 This spell sends a person or object forward in time. For game purposes, the target disappears in an instant and reappears in the same place sometime later. While the target does not perceive any period of time passing and only sees his environment shift suddenly, those around him at the moment of casting see him disappear completely. The caster may choose how much time ahead the target jumps, within the limits defined by the level of the spell. However, there is an additional limit to its effects. There are certain moments in history, marking important events, which are spiritually impossible to avoid. Therefore, if the Time Jump passes through one of them, the spell ends ahead of schedule and the target returns to reality at that moment. The target can resist the spell by passing a MR check with a difficulty determined by the level of the spell.":

The caster may choose how much time ahead the target jumps, within the limits defined by the level of the spell. However, there is an additional limit to its effects. There are certain moments in history, marking important events, which are spiritually impossible to avoid. Therefore, if the Time Jump passes through one of them, the spell ends ahead of schedule and the target returns to reality at that moment. The target can resist the spell by passing a MR check with a difficulty determined by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row:
        - cell "Temporal Loop"
        - cell "Time"
        - cell "84"
        - cell

      - row:
        - cell "Overlap Time"
        - cell "Time"
        - cell "94"
        - cell

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Dream Stalker Umbra 44 This spell allows the wizard to convert his victim’s dreams into a prison where his soul is trapped in an endless nightmare. This spell must be cast on an individual who is dreaming, and if he fails his MR or PsR check, he cannot wake up until the spell is ended.         - cell "Void"

      - row "Void Wave Void 14 This spell releases a small wave of void capable of disintegrating any supernatural protection.         - cell "Void Wave"
        - cell "Void"
        - cell "14"
        - cell "This spell releases a small wave of void capable of disintegrating any supernatural protection. It causes no damage to enemies, but has devastating effects when directed against a supernatural shield. A Void Wave is invisible to the naked eye, except to those able to see magic."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Void Shield Void 24 This spell creates a barrier that absorbs particles and converts all attacks received into nothingness. Besides having Life Points like a normal supernatural shield, the barrier automatically cancels any supernatural power that strikes it if the attacker cannot pass a Willpower or Power check with a difficulty determined by the level of the spell. That is, if an opponent launches an Attack or Spiritual spell against a wizard who is protected behind an Intermediate level Void Shield and the wizard makes a successful defense, the attacker must pass a Willpower or Power 16 check or his attack is canceled without causing any damage to the shield. Similarly, any inanimate physical object stopped by the shield must pass a PhR check or be destroyed immediately.":
        - cell "Void Shield"
        - cell "Void"
        - cell "24"

That is, if an opponent launches an Attack or Spiritual spell against a wizard who is protected behind an Intermediate level Void Shield and the wizard makes a successful defense, the attacker must pass a Willpower or Power 16 check or his attack is canceled without causing any damage to the shield. Similarly, any inanimate physical object stopped by the shield must pass a PhR check or be destroyed immediately."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Reality Vortex Void 34 This ability creates an area full of millions of microscopic voids around the caster. Any individual who is in it for more than a turn must pass a MR check each turn or suffer damage equal to half the degree of failure, lose Ki points equal to half the degree of failure, and lose Zeon points equal to the degree of failure. This spell does not discriminate between people within the vortex, and affects everyone, including the caster. The effect is stationary once cast.":

        - cell "Void"
        - cell "34"

This spell does not discriminate between people within the vortex, and affects everyone, including the caster. The effect is stationary once cast."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Black Blade of Doom Void 44 This spell summons the void in its purest form, shaping a crackling blade of power in the caster’s hands. This weapon can be wielded to make attacks with the Cut Attack Type using the caster’s Magic Projection or Attack Ability and completely ignores all Armor Types. The wizard can keep the blade active to make further attacks in later turns, but each turn he wishes to do so, he must pass a Willpower check against a difficulty of 12 or lose control of the sword. Every time he passes, the difficulty of the next check increases by 1. If he fails, the sword turns against him, attacking with his best Attack Ability +50, and then disappears.":

        - cell "Void"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Black Spots Void 64 This spell creates a vortex around the wizard filled with vortices of nothingness that move up and down chaotically. Anyone within that area must pass an Athletics, Acrobatics, or Defense check against a difficulty set by the spell level or collide with a black spot. If this occurs, the character must pass a MR or PhR check with a difficulty determined by the spell level or suffer damage equal to half the degree of failure, lose Ki points equal to half the degree of failure, and lose Zeon points equal to the degree of failure. It is impossible to designate targets for this spell; everyone in the area is affected, including the caster. Once cast, the area of effect moves with the caster; he is always at the center of it.":

        - cell "Void"

    - rowgroup:

Anyone who walks through the door will automatically cease to exist without any possible Resistance check. Only beings with Gnosis 35 or more, objects of equivalent Presence, or individuals who are somehow protected against the void can survive there, but even they will slowly lose points from their Power Characteristic at a rate of 1 per day, which recuperate at the same rate once the individuals are out of the void. Naturally, someone within the portal is virtually immune to any kind of attack, since attacks will dissipate into the void. The dimensions of the portal are determined by the level of the spell.":

The Strength value of the binding once struck depends on the material used. For example, thick rope would have a Strength value of 10, and metallic chains a value of 12.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Net Free Access 20-30 This spell creates high density magical fibers, which act as a very sticky web. It has a number of uses such as blocking an area, or throwing directly on individuals. Any creature within its radius is trapped with a strength value of 10 for the spell. The net also has a pool of life points it can sustain damage with before breaking, with an AT of 4. Being supernatural substance, the net can only be damaged by Heat or other attacks capable of damaging energy.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "20-30"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Eliminate Dreams Free Access 30-40 It suppresses the ability of an individual to dream. If cast on a target in the wake, they will be immediately expelled to the real world. The spell can be resisted with a successful MR check, although if failed it can only be repeated once a day.":

        - cell "Free Access"

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "30-40"
        - cell "Destroy an active spell whose Zeon value does not exceed that indicated by the spell's level when cast."
    - rowgroup:
      - row:
        - cell "Extend Presence"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "30-40"
        - cell "The caster can separate their soul from their body at will, allowing them to act remotely as if they were truly there. While the caster is separated, they will not be able to move, but will be able to make their soul return to their body whenever desired without having to end the spell. The expanded presence can touch physical objects and even attack, but it will be invisible to the human eye; It is only possible to notice it through the use of Ki detection, the ability to see spirits, or some other means of seeing ethereal bodies (although if their presence is holding something, that object is perfectly visible). The presence is intangible and can only be damaged by attacks capable of damaging energy. Any loss of life points will also affect the caster’s body. The maximum distance that the presence can separate from the body is determined by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Heal Diseases Free Access 30-40 Automatically heals any disease up to a certain disease level. This can affect a number of individuals, as long as the sum of their presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell.":
        - cell "Heal Diseases"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "30-40"
        - cell "Automatically heals any disease up to a certain disease level. This can affect a number of individuals, as long as the sum of their presence is within the limit set by the level of the spell."

      - row "Invoke Aggressiveness Free Access 30-40 Automatically increases the aggressiveness of all living beings close to the caster, should they fail an MR check.         - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "30-40"
        - cell "Automatically increases the aggressiveness of all living beings close to the caster, should they fail an MR check. Those affected are subject to the Rage state and act in a violent manner toward any individual or thing targetable by their anger."

    - rowgroup:

Purification only affects harmful elements of a body that do not belong to it.     - rowgroup:
      - row "True Close Free Access 30-40 Through this spell, the caster automatically obstructs any type of door, shutter, or window. If for example, it is cast upon an open door, it will close by itself. If upon the latch, it will physically bar the exit. While the spell is maintained, it is not possible to open the door physically, and only mystical effects or destruction will open it. The maximum presence of the opening affected is determined by the level of the spell.":

        - cell "Free Access"

The maximum presence of the opening affected is determined by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Absorb Information Free Access 40-50 The caster absorbs written or visual knowledge within sight or reach. Thus, it is possible for example, to read a book in a few seconds by touching its cover and remember the details as if it had been perused carefully. A character who tries to remember information memorized using this spell decreases the difficulty of any Memorize check by 2 degrees.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "40-50"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Alter Energy Free Access 40-50 The caster can transform intensities of any kind of elemental intensity into another, such as converting fire into electricity.         - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "40-50"

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "40-50"

    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Curse Free Access 40-50 Causes a series of negative conditions on the person it affects. It causes misfortune when certain actions are performed by the victim: maintaining a romantic relationship, writing poetry, helping someone… The caster decides what exactly the action is, as well as it’s consequences, within the limit set by the level of the spell. By it’s nature, the spell does not influence only those directly targeted, but also others who interact with them if they enter the designation of the curse. In this case, every individual is entitled to their own MR check. For example, a young girl who is interested in forming a relationship with a boy affected by a curse that prevents others from falling in love with him, would feel discouraged from this action should she fail the MR of the spell. New resistance checks may only be performed when the condition is met an additional time."':
        - cell "Curse"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "40-50"
        - 'cell "Causes a series of negative conditions on the person it affects. It causes misfortune when certain actions are performed by the victim: maintaining a romantic relationship, writing poetry, helping someone… The caster decides what exactly the action is, as well as it’s consequences, within the limit set by the level of the spell. By it’s nature, the spell does not influence only those directly targeted, but also others who interact with them if they enter the designation of the curse. In this case, every individual is entitled to their own MR check. For example, a young girl who is interested in forming a relationship with a boy affected by a curse that prevents others from falling in love with him, would feel discouraged from this action should she fail the MR of the spell. New resistance checks may only be performed when the condition is met an additional time."'
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Friendship Free Access 40-50 Creates a bond of friendship towards another person designated by the caster. Keep in mind the victim does not become stupid, so they do not blindly obey their ‘friend’, but instead act according to their personality. To overcome the effects of this spell, a successful MR or PsR check is required. Subsequently, the resistance roll is repeated only once a day, or when something may cast doubt upon the companionship this spell creates.":
        - cell "Friendship"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "40-50"
        - cell "Creates a bond of friendship towards another person designated by the caster. To overcome the effects of this spell, a successful MR or PsR check is required. Subsequently, the resistance roll is repeated only once a day, or when something may cast doubt upon the companionship this spell creates."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Quick Transport Free Access 40-50 Transport the target up to a specified distance. This allows the transported individual to pass through solid matter, provided it is not considered an energy barrier. Additionally, they cannot be transported to a place of a different nature than the one they’re currently in (for example, by making someone appear in the air).":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Read Minds Free Access 40-50 Allows the caster to delve into the thoughts or memories of an individual. It is at the GM’s discretion to decide the number of combat turns needed to find the desired information, depending on how deeply hidden it is in the character’s memory. The affected can resist with either an MR or PsR check. As long as the caster can read the intentions of their victim, he receives a +30 bonus to any opposed check against the target.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "40-50"

    - rowgroup:

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Acid Cloud Free Access 50-60 Forms a corrosive cloud, which melts any material by mere contact. All bodies in contact with the cloud must succeed at a PhR check or lose an amount of life points equal to the level of failure. If an object fails this check by 50 points or more, it loses 1 level of quality. Each turn that a body is exposed to the cloud and fails the associated resistance check, it applies a cumulative -10 penalty to subsequent checks. The caster can move the cloud at a speed determined by the level of the spell. All bodies inside it’s area of effect the turn after it is formed are affected. Naturally, it is not possible to distinguish targets inside the spell’s area, including the caster themselves.":
        - cell "Acid Cloud"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "50-60"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Merge With Body Free Access 50-60 Merges one or more objects such as weapons, armor, lock-picks, etc. with the individual designated by the caster, with a presence limit determined by the level of the spell. The object remains hidden under any visible form desired, such as a scar or tattoo, until such time as the person containing it decides to remove it. While hidden, the carrier will not have any penalty applied for the object’s weight or size, but cannot use it for it’s benefits. It is not possible to merge something that exceeds more than ten times the size of the person acting as the container. Separating the object and externalizing it requires a full combat turn.":

        - cell "Free Access"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Magic Weapon Free Access 60-70 Creates a magical weapon from pure magic. The weapon is considered of the quality determined by the level of the spell, and can innately damage energy.":
        - cell "Magic Weapon"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "60-70"
        - cell "Creates a magical weapon from pure magic. The weapon is considered of the quality determined by the level of the spell, and can innately damage energy."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Minor Alteration Free Access 60-70 Allows the caster to mold the shape of inorganic matter, transmuting an object into one of similar shape and presence. For example, it is possible to convert a harpoon to a spear, as they are both similarly shaped and have the same presence. However, the same harpoon could not be molded into a two-handed sword, as their presence and shape are considerably different.":

In Game terms, this spell must be cast on a subject or place infected with a disease no greater than the limit imposed by the level of the spell, and by doing so automatically spreads it to all individuals within it’s area of effect. The people affected are entitled to a DR against the disease to avoid it’s effects. The only condition to be affected is to be within the area when it is cast.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "60-70"

The people affected are entitled to a DR against the disease to avoid it’s effects. The only condition to be affected is to be within the area when it is cast."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Rejection Free Access 60-70 Enchants a physical body with magical energy, producing a strong rejection to anyone who touches it. The spell causes an impact on anyone coming into contact with the subject, and the spell has it’s own Strength value for this purpose. As in similar scenarios, the GM must determine the damage suffered by the character pushed by the impact, depending on the difference in opposed check and the elements in the environment. The maximum presence of the affected subject cannot exceed the limit set by the level of the spell.":

        - cell "Free Access"

The maximum presence of the affected subject cannot exceed the limit set by the level of the spell."

        - cell "Resist"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "60-70"

      - 'row "Weakness Free Access 60-70 The target of the spell is subject to the state of Weakness as explained in Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Chapter 14: States and Accidents."':

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Containment Free Access 70-80 Enchants a certain place by making any creature that enters or is already inside unable to leave it’s border unless they succeed at an MR check. Although they can move within the interior area freely, it will be completely impossible to cross outside the barrier. Once a subject fails to resist, they are not entitled to any new resistance check for as long as the spell is maintained, unless for some reason their resistance score increases. The only condition to be affected is simply to be inside the area any time after the turn it is cast. For this reason, the caster will be unable to choose targets affected in the area, and will themselves be contained if they remain in the area.":
        - cell "Containment"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "70-80"
        - cell "Enchants a certain place by making any creature that enters or is already inside unable to leave it’s border unless they succeed at an MR check. The only condition to be affected is simply to be inside the area any time after the turn it is cast. For this reason, the caster will be unable to choose targets affected in the area, and will themselves be contained if they remain in the area."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Defensive Erudition Free Access 70-80 Increases the caster’s own defensive magic projection. Characters can only be affected by one Defensive Erudition at a time.":
        - cell "Defensive Erudition"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "70-80"
        - cell "Increases the caster’s own defensive magic projection. Characters can only be affected by one Defensive Erudition at a time."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Deflect Trajectory Free Access 70-80 Modifies the trajectory of a moving body, redirecting it in the way the caster wishes. Affects any amount of mass, whether small like an arrow or as large as a moving ship. Keep in mind it does not allow movement of a static body, but only modifying the route that one already in motion takes. This may also be used as a defense against material attacks, such as slashes or projectiles. In the event the caster achieves a successful defense with magic projection, they may redirect the attack to another foe. The effects of this spell in either case can be avoided by a successful MR check.":
        - cell "Deflect Trajectory"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "70-80"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Detection Mark Free Access 70-80 Enchants a person, object, or place, allowing the caster to attune their senses to it. In this way, the caster may use their perception abilities as if present with the subject, as long as their distance is no greater than the limit set by the level of the spell. In addition, the caster is always aware of where the mark is in relation to their own location. Living beings and supernatural objects being targeted by this spell may attempt an MR check to avoid it’s effects.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "70-80"

In addition, the caster is always aware of where the mark is in relation to their own location. Only animate beings and constructs endowed with supernatural characteristics are allowed to attempt this resistance.":

Only animate beings and constructs endowed with supernatural characteristics are allowed to attempt this resistance."

      - row "Offensive Erudition Free Access 70-80 Increases the caster’s offensive magic projection. Only one Offensive Erudition spell may affect a character at a time.":
        - cell "Offensive Erudition"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "70-80"
        - cell "Increases the caster’s offensive magic projection. Only one Offensive Erudition spell may affect a character at a time."
    - rowgroup:
      - 'row "Perfect Target Free Access 70-80 Magically enchants a conventional projectile so that, once shot, it will reach its target inescapably. The projectile still uses the ability of it’s launcher, and if used to attack an individual, it is defended against using the conventional rules for projectile defense. However, the supernatural spell’s power gives the attacker a bonus to their roll, allowing them to ignore any penalty of Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Table 44: Projectile Modifiers, as well as distance to the target. In other words, it will always reach it’s target, no matter where the launcher is. Perfect Target must be used in the same combat turn as the projectile is launched."':
        - cell "Perfect Target"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "70-80"
        - 'cell "Magically enchants a conventional projectile so that, once shot, it will reach its target inescapably. The projectile still uses the ability of it’s launcher, and if used to attack an individual, it is defended against using the conventional rules for projectile defense. However, the supernatural spell’s power gives the attacker a bonus to their roll, allowing them to ignore any penalty of Anima: Beyond Fantasy – Table 44: Projectile Modifiers, as well as distance to the target. In other words, it will always reach it’s target, no matter where the launcher is. Perfect Target must be used in the same combat turn as the projectile is launched."'
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Stall Spell Free Access 70-80 Allows a second spell to be ‘stored’ inside an object, so that it may be used by other sorcerers. Stall spell must be cast at the same time as the intended spell, so that the second spell instead of functioning normally, will be stuck inside something. The stalled spell may be activated later, by any sorcerer who enters the matching Zeonic cost into the container as when it was cast. The holder of the object will control the spell as if they were the caster, and may even pay it’s maintenance if applicable. The stalled spell may be used more than once, provided the holder pays the same cost each time. As an example; an archmage has placed a fireball spell at Base grade inside of a cane. From that moment on, any character that possesses that cane may pay the 50 Zeon to trigger a fireball.":
        - cell "Stall Spell"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "70-80"
        - cell "Allows a second spell to be ‘stored’ inside an object, so that it may be used by other sorcerers. Stall spell must be cast at the same time as the intended spell, so that the second spell instead of functioning normally, will be stuck inside something. The stalled spell may be activated later, by any sorcerer who enters the matching Zeonic cost into the container as when it was cast. The holder of the object will control the spell as if they were the caster, and may even pay it’s maintenance if applicable. The stalled spell may be used more than once, provided the holder pays the same cost each time. As an example; an archmage has placed a fireball spell at Base grade inside of a cane. From that moment on, any character that possesses that cane may pay the 50 Zeon to trigger a fireball."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Uselessness Free Access 70-80 The target becomes clumsy and unable to perform physical maneuvers. The target must succeed at an MR check or suffer a penalty to all physical actions equivalent to the level of failure. Naturally this penalty does not apply to magical, psychic, or perceptual abilities.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "70-80"
        - cell "The target becomes clumsy and unable to perform physical maneuvers. The target must succeed at an MR check or suffer a penalty to all physical actions equivalent to the level of failure. Naturally this penalty does not apply to magical, psychic, or perceptual abilities."

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "80-90"
        - cell "Automatically destroy a magic item. The caster can choose whether the object is completely destroyed or simply stripped of it’s supernatural properties."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Eliminate Needs Free Access 80-90 Completely eliminates any physical needs of an individual. In game terms, as long as the spell is maintained, the subject will not be affected by the effects of hunger, fatigue, or sleep, completely ignoring any penalties caused by them (or by their lack of them). The character can continue to spend fatigue points to increase their physical actions, and are recovered at a rate of 1 point per hour passively, and does not suffer negative effects from very low fatigue points.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "80-90"

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Gate Free Access 80-90 Creates a magical portal between two distant places. Anyone crossing it will move instantly to the other opening. This spell is fixed in place once cast at both the connecting points, so the caster cannot change it’s location later. The caster may also decide when casting if it works in both directions, or only transports one way. The portals can only transmit a maximum amount of presence per day; this value, along with the size of the portal opening as well as the maximum distance that can separate the points are determined by the level of the spell.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "80-90"

The portals can only transmit a maximum amount of presence per day; this value, along with the size of the portal opening as well as the maximum distance that can separate the points are determined by the level of the spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Immortality Free Access 80-90 While this spell remains active, the caster or their target does not age nor suffer the effects of the passage of time. Thus, they will be an immortal being, except in the face of a violent death. If this spell ceases to be maintained or is destroyed, the individual recovers their age at a very rapid rate.":

    - rowgroup:
      - row "Location Free Access 80-90 Allows the caster to find a person, object, or place, and know it’s exact location at the time, provided it’s within the distance set by the level of the spell. The caster can look for anything concrete, but not using subjective qualities. For example, the caster may not use this spell to locate his brother’s killer unless he knows exactly who that is. To avoid detection, people or qualifying objects must succeed at an MR check. Large places will apply a -40 to this roll.":

      - row "Physical Immunity Free Access 80-90 The designated individual or object becomes completely invulnerable to any damage that is not capable of damaging energy.         - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "80-90"
        - cell "Returns an active spell against it’s originator, or any other target chosen. The returned spell uses the original projection of the person who executed it. This is an automatic effect, and so no resistance is allowed."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Steal Spell Free Access 80-90 Subtracts the magical essence of a spell, cutting the ties with the sorcerer who cast it and linking it to another. In game terms, the caster takes control of a spell that is not theirs, controlling it from that moment onward as if they had cast it themselves. The stolen spell may not have a Zeonic value greater than the limit set by the level of this spell, and it is only possible to steal those spells that have maintenance; not spells whose effects are immediate and automatic. The original caster may attempt to resist these effects by succeeding at an MR check.":
        - cell "Steal Spell"
        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "80-90"
        - cell "Subtracts the magical essence of a spell, cutting the ties with the sorcerer who cast it and linking it to another. The stolen spell may not have a Zeonic value greater than the limit set by the level of this spell, and it is only possible to steal those spells that have maintenance; not spells whose effects are immediate and automatic. The original caster may attempt to resist these effects by succeeding at an MR check."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Teletransportation Free Access 80-90 The caster or their targets is teleported to another position.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Imitate Spells Free Access 90-100 Allows the caster to imitate a spell that is in their presence. Spells cast in the same turn as Imitate Spells, as well as those maintained during that turn, can be imitated. This can even apply to spells of High or Divine magic, without the usual need for the caster to have superior Gnosis to cast them. The caster chooses whether to use their own magic projection for the imitated spell, or the value of the original caster. The maximum Zeonic value for imitated spells is determined by the level of this spell when cast. Maintenance is as per the imitated spell.":

        - cell "Free Access"
        - cell "90-100"
        - cell "Allows the caster to imitate a spell that is in their presence. Spells cast in the same turn as Imitate Spells, as well as those maintained during that turn, can be imitated. This can even apply to spells of High or Divine magic, without the usual need for the caster to have superior Gnosis to cast them. The caster chooses whether to use their own magic projection for the imitated spell, or the value of the original caster. The maximum Zeonic value for imitated spells is determined by the level of this spell when cast. Maintenance is as per the imitated spell."
    - rowgroup:
      - row "Innate Magic Free Access 90-100 Exponentially increases the environmental magical potential of an area, thus increasing the capacity for sorcerers to use innate magic.     - rowgroup:
      - row "Link Maintenance Free Access 90-100 Allows the caster to link the maintenance payment for one of their spells to another individual or object, forcing the spending of the new target’s Zeon in an unconscious way. This link can only be used on people who possess the Gift, or upon magical beings or objects, which can avoid being affected with a successful MR check. If the linked individual does not realize they are being targeted by this spell, they are not entitled to a new resistance check. If noticed, however, a new resistance check is able to be performed depending on the type of spell linked. In the case of daily maintenance spells a check is allowed each day, and for all others a new check is allowed every five rounds. The moment that a resistance is successful or if the linked target runs out of Zeon, the effect of the link disappears forever.":

While maintained, all spells of the caster are considered to have ‘fake’ Zeon points added to the Zeonic value of their spells. Since these points are not real, they do not add any additional effects or increase the casting level of the spells. They merely increase their value for the purposes of comparing to abilities that affect and destroy spells. If, for example, the caster uses Strengthen Magic in the base level (+50 points), and uses the spell Create Light at base level (50 Zeonic value), the potential of the Light spell would not be increased to any higher level, but a second sorcerer using Destroy Magic at base grade (50 Zeon limit) would not be able to destroy the light spell.":
